> **OWNER FINAL PRIMITIVE — THIRD SUPERSESSION (2026-09-30):** Neither earlier `0.5169026601` (block-wise credits to both truths from one called slot) nor `0.5241800568` (whole-chromosome slot-to-truth assignment) is the per-locus genotype-pair verdict. The product is a PER-LOCUS unordered PAIR; score the called pair against the two *alignment-attested* local truth alleles by the injective 2×2 best assignment, with an empty call paying its assigned truth allele's FULL length. Sum local edit distances over attested loci. Bracket loci without a full unique SK1 homolog; never infer an empty SK1 truth allele from missing territory pieces. ## chrVI causal gate and wall correction (fleet in progress)
The TWO-ROUTE truth-domain check, not the assessment's sequence self-test, decides why the diploid call fails. The existing Stage-1 **existential truth-pair predicate** and genotype exact-piece fields independently agree for chrVI: S288C expressed **29/29**, SK1 expressed **2/29** (loci 1–2), BOTH expressed **2/29**. The `[truth-pieces]` run diagnostic records no SK1 copy piece in the remaining 27 reference-windowed territory loci: there is no two-route truth pair to score there. The 14/29 exact *sequence-homolog assessment* windows are an independent coverage set, NOT evidence of pair expressibility; none of these 14 windows is a truth-pair-domain window. Consequently the requested `IMPG_LOSS_DECOMP_LOCUS` comparison is **inapplicable** on an exact-2x2 chrVI window: that existing flag decomposes a single-allele loss only; instrumenting the pair against a missing second truth row would invent a comparison. The domain gap is itself the bounded verdict. Whole-route model-of-record m1: truth pair +154,160.71 nats; selected haploid -63,392.75; **pair priced +217,553.46 nats worse** (haploid_truth reference -81,063.70, not the selected chain). The global preference is consistent with the dominant representational gap, not evidence that the diploid machinery is broken. Where BOTH are expressible (2/29), the objective can still favor a single; this check does not claim that the domain gap explains those two loci.

**chrVI dosage honesty:** every one of 29 genotype calls emits a SINGLE dosage class with dosage **2**, every selected second slot is literally `empty-slot2`; the true mixture is **1.2 S288C / 0.8 SK1**. The dosage column does NOT flag the missed second component as an unknown: it emits the wrong one-class, two-copy readout; its singleton credible-set rows have `credible_set_mass: null` (not a calibrated certainty claim). Sequence-nearest labels on 14 attested windows: 12 assign both copies to S288C, 2 both to SK1; neither recovers the two nonzero dosages. This is reference-windowed domain-frame debt, not a diploid-track execution failure: see `docs/syng-gmem-bwt/partition-native-spine.md`'s partition/territory-vs-window duality.

**Wall arithmetic correction to inherited §2.34:** the actual sum of its 17 printed measured per-component walls is **16,112 seconds = 4.4756 hours (4.48h)**; the text's old `~2.6h` and `~17,600s` are BOTH arithmetic errors. Largest single run 3,790s (63m10s) remains true. The parallel lane's apparent faster wall is not a safe alternative: it has the documented reference-rescore race. Summing recorded rows, not a mental estimate, is now the rule for all wall totals.

## Forced-diploid diagnostic — PRE-REGISTERED before execution
The owner-approved diagnostic constrains the *selected* chain to the existing two-real-slot DP track (`IMPG_FORCE_DIPLOID_DIAGNOSTIC=1`); haploid DP may still be computed as an observational comparison but **cannot be selected**. No objective, tables, pruning, dosage formula, or reference truth is changed. This flag is diagnostic-only; normal runs remain bit-identical. Before running chrI and chrVI, the predicted observations are: **(i)** chrI's 17 truth-pair-in-domain windows select the exact S288C+SK1 pair (whether-pair preference, not which-pair ranking, is the defect); **(ii)** chrVI's 27 SK1-inexpressible windows fill with confident *wrong* second slots if empty slots are denied; **(iii)** the dosage machinery remains uncalibrated to the true 1.2/0.8 because the feature-window mass is pooled. Decision test: selected route indices against exact truth-pair alleles at each in-domain locus; on inexpressible loci, report second-slot source/identity, confidence fields without promoting `null` masses to posterior probabilities; dosage from actual reported genotype columns and selected pair class counts. Preserve the 17-row pre-run delta unchanged. The production baseline for contrast is chrI 17/21 and chrVI 2/29 truth-pair-in-domain, both selected haploid; this is the pre-run record, not a post-hoc hypothesis.

### Forced-diploid diagnostic: chrI outcome (first run)
`IMPG_FORCE_DIPLOID_DIAGNOSTIC=1` is an env-gated **selection restriction**: the existing diploid DP has only two real slots; the haploid DP is still computed observationally, but its empty-slot route is never selected. chrI **exit 0 / 179s**, selected ploidy diploid, both slots real at all 21 windows. The 17 truth-pair-in-domain loci **match the exact truth pair 0/17** by source-named genotype allele IDs; the forced run's truth allele IDs are identical to the baseline's. Prediction (i) (true pair chosen when the whether-pair decision is taken away) is **REFUTED**. On 10 uniquely attested homolog windows, best 2x2 sequence accuracy **0.631852/10**; H1/H2 assignment flips each **1 over 7 adjacent attested boundaries**. The 11 correspondence brackets remain separate. Truth-pair self-test remains **1.0/0 over 10**. The genotype dosage columns **still say one class of 2 at all 21 windows**, rather than 1.2/0.8 — a locally called-pair output not recomputed from the forced selected chain. This diagnostic is NOT evidence that the which-pair ranking is sound: it instead produces many wrong real second slots even where the exact truth pair exists. chrVI still running serially.

### Speed directive correction (measured BEFORE draft surgery)
The 3,178s chrIV diploid wall decomposes into **2,311.19s phasing.rescore, 316.76s transition_costs INCLUDING drafts, 0.29s haploid DP, and 550.05s other**. Eliminating the entire transition stage would only reach 2,861s, not the originally requested 65–420s class. Jemalloc 5.3.0 preload chrIV64 exits **0 / 919s**, with every rescore product field, both DP best totals, and 166 boundary stats bit-identical. **CRITICAL sampling gap:** jemalloc's JSON `rss_peak_bytes=31,104,483,328 B` is a STAGE/periodic-checkpoint reading AFTER its eager reclamation; independent 5-second `/proc` RSS poller captured a transient **46,901,632 KiB = 48,027,271,168 B** during the parallel oracle. The run DID NOT peak at 31GB. Use external peaks consistently for the comparator:

Safety guard still succeeded on all these runs, but do not substitute its stage maximum for external peak.

### Default width-16 first p1 gate: safety failure found and bounded
The initial production-default width16 binary passed chrMT (exit 0 / 69s; all rescore product columns and both DP totals bit-identical), but the context-aware p1 baseline is **4-core**. Its first run failed closed **exit 101 / 496s** during oracle work: `src/agc_index.rs:170` panicked index 7–14 out of bounds on the five per-thread decompressor slots allocated as `rayon::current_num_threads()+1` for the GLOBAL 4-core pool. The new scoped pool16 had issued thread indices > global pool size. This was a genuine compatibility bug, not a product answer; NO p1 identity claim on that run. Narrow fix: bound the new scoped oracle pool to `min(requested16, rayon::current_num_threads())` with AGC slot rationale in source. This leaves chrIV at 16, p1 at its original 4, and guarantees scoped Rayon indices fit AGC's allocation. Rebuild and repeat chrMT+p1 full product gates, then commit; no broad AGC architecture change and no silent parser exception. The failed run's exit/error/wall are retained in `run-interface-p1` runner history (logs may be overwritten on successful retry; this report is the diagnostic record).

### Corrected scoped oracle width default: chrMT + context-p1 exactness PASS
The final candidate is only 15 source-code added lines in `examples/panel_route_spine/phasing.rs`: build a scoped Rayon pool for `haploid_allele_losses` with `min(requested default 16, rayon::current_num_threads())`. This explicit global-width ceiling is REQUIRED because `AgcIndex` sizes per-thread decompressors from the GLOBAL Rayon width and indexes by CURRENT scoped worker index. All other stages retain their existing width, and `IMPG_HAPLOID_ORACLE_THREADS` can override for reproducible diagnostics (still bounded by global width). The first unbounded p1 exit101 was retained as a failure gate; this corrected binary was rebuilt and re-gated:
- chrMT exits **0 / 71s**, every selected/reference `spine.phasing.rescore` product column (excluding elapsed wall) and DP totals bit-identical to old `run-smoke2-chrMT`.
- Context-aware 4-core p1 exits **0 / 577s**; ALL rescore product columns (once-per-material/refund, three references, pooled evaluations, selected route, junction), both DP totals, and all 14 legacy boundary stats bit-identical to `run-p1-ladder` baseline. Rescore **93.8967→6.1731s**. RSS 19,042,295,808 B (the p1 4-core lane stays 4 workers by the AGC safety ceiling; there is no claim of a p1 RAM reduction). Product preserved under `run-width16bounded-p1.*` in the context directory.

The **final compiled binary** is now running guarded chrIV 64-core with default scoped 16-worker oracle for the production-form RAM+wall+product receipt. Prior diagnostic explicit width16 chrIV provided 771s / external peak39,007,960KiB; it was NOT the final compiled production form, so wait for this final run before committing the scoped default as fully measured. ## OWNER OVERRIDE — 2026-09-30: the previous diploid yardstick is SUPERSEDED
**Read this section before the historical 17-row table or forced-diploid exact-pair verdicts above.** The `score-diploid.py` criterion required a single full-length unique SK1 placement for every S288C partition. Its 554 brackets, 502/1307 exact truth-pair-domain tally, and forced chrI 0/17/chrVI 0/2 exact allele-ID matches are *properties of that reference-windowed yardstick*, not a valid sequence-distance verdict against the simulated diploid genome. The owner requires the SAME alignment-native currency as the haploid scoreboard, extended to two physical truth haplotypes, with phase/crossovers measured separately. Do not erase the old records: label them explicitly MIS-CALIBRATED sequence yardsticks, keep them as structural diagnostics only, and do not turn an empty selected slot into undefined pair sequence error. Dosage remains separately wrong, but it does not by itself measure sequence disagreement.

### (a) BOUNDED ASSESSMENT REBUILD FORM — existing inferred outputs only, no production reruns
Inputs are the two actual FASTA simulation truth paths `S288C#0#<component>` and `SK1#0#<component>`, the saved `run-{smoke2|fleet}-<component>.log` selected routes, and the source FASTA/AGC spell already used by `score-diploid.py`. Align each SPOKEN inferred slot's oriented sequence blocks against BOTH complete truth haplotypes, not against a required 1:1 projected window: mappy assembly alignments allow supplementary mappings, inversions and fragmented chrMT placements; edlib path/CIGAR verifies mismatches, insertions and deletions on the aligned local block. A deterministic best non-overlapping block assignment chooses truth haplotype/strand per aligned query interval by sequence agreement, allowing the assignment to change at crossover/recombination points rather than charging an otherwise correct block as a sequence mutation. Count unique correct covered truth bases under each truth slot (deduplicate overlapping same-truth placements), plus substitutions/deletions and query-only insertions separately; truth bases lacking any inferred coverage and unaligned query bases remain explicit omissions/extras, not free or silently bracketed. Report sequence distance normalized to the TWO truth-path lengths with the exact numerator and denominator, observed coverage, unaligned/repetitive ambiguity and splice/breakpoint accounting; report crossover/switch transitions and inferred copy/dosage/empty-slot claims in DISTINCT columns. Test the same truth routes against themselves: two-truth self distance=0, no switches and full coverage through chrI inversions/chrMT fragmentation; test swapped truth slots: distance=0 with a phase permutation but no sequence mutation. A single correct homolog should NOT spuriously get full diploid sequence credit: its absent second homolog remains explicit uncovered truth sequence. Keep legacy assessment JSON/report untouched; write source-named `distance-*` sidecars and one aggregate 17-row score, with original run wall references (no new production work).

This FORM fixes the denominator, but two details require decision before implementation: how to charge unmapped/unaligned bases (the proposed full-truth denominator charges missing bases and reports extra query insertions) and whether a one-slot chain's chromosome-to-chromosome switches are counted as *phase switches* even though no second slot exists (proposed: separately label ancestry/crossover on the single chain, not a fabricated two-haplotype phase switch). No sequence distance from the old bracket count or exact allele-ID predicate may substitute for this alignment score.

### (b) KNEE CUT DESIGN — APPROVAL REQUEST, NOT IMPLEMENTED
**Input mass must be defined before any cut.** Per-locus candidate states have existing finite m1 losses; normalize `log_mass_i = -(loss_i-min_loss) - logsumexp_j(-(loss_j-min_loss))` on the COMPLETE enumerable state set. For a transition pair, the ranking must include the upstream DP forward cost, pair/owner/restricted charge, and downstream suffix lower bound or a genuinely computed forward-backward log marginal; bare pair count, flank multiplicity or unnormalized transition cost is NOT probability mass. For pooled compositions/drafts, aggregate the bounded mass of all realizing state pairs without double counting. Sort descending by this mass, retain prefix through the unique curve-derived knee (proposal: maximal perpendicular distance of cumulative log-mass from its endpoint chord; tie -> retain largest prefix; flat/no finite cliff -> retain ALL), plus every exact incumbent/argmin that is not already in the prefix. Persist per-layer `retained_mass`, `omitted_mass_upper_bound`, first omitted score/loss lower bound, number retained/discarded and the knee index in product columns; tie/flat/no certified tail => no pruning. Path-level bound must propagate excluded-state/pair mass through DP using proven per-boundary suffix bounds, not sum independent unnormalized local tails. Final argmin and objective must be compared against the uncut baseline at chrMT/p1; no new untuned mass bound may be asserted merely from rank. Safety gates check (1) the selected argmin was in the prefix BEFORE force-retaining it (otherwise knee fails and that boundary remains uncut); (2) truthful two-haplotype sequence distance and switches from assessment side independently; (3) correct once-per-material m1, references, junction census and retained-tail fields; (4) external 5s RSS, staged wall and distribution/knee compression at chrMT/p1 then chrIV/chrVII (42M/29M pair lanes). Abort/revert if cut loses argmin, tail bound is uncertified, reference fields change without explicit approval or wall/RSS bar fails. No threshold tuned to fit one chromosome; 100–300s/component, GB-class RAM and ~17,000s genome wall are the owner's measurable targets, NOT achieved by the previous width-only fix.

**Circularity that approval must resolve:** scoring all 42M exact pair charges and building all draft/composition profiles before deriving their exact posterior masses yields no transition-work or peak-RSS gain. The only exact safe pre-enumeration knee needs a cheap, certified upper envelope for every unenumerated pair's global mass that accounts for potentially negative restricted charges and sharing, or a two-pass observation index with a bound; current DP tables do not expose that certificate. A mass curve computed AFTER all pairs is diagnostic only, not an approved structural memory/speed fix. Request owner approval for a specifically defined observable mass, exact tail certificate and cheap pre-enumeration bound before implementing a production knee; do not silently substitute local losses for transition posteriors or promise the argmin cannot lie beyond a heuristic cliff.

The new **assessment-only** `genome/instrumented/score-diploid-distance.py`, serial `run-diploid-distance.sh`, and source-named three-test suite scored the EXISTING 17 completed `run-smoke2`/`run-fleet` outputs, with no new product runs, no proportional SK1 projection and no full-unique-window bracket criterion. ### (b) Diagnostic knee-curve implementation: exact post-charge evidence only
The corrected assessment gate is committed `38697eb` and is the new sequence-distance record. As supervisor approved, a diagnostic-only `IMPG_KNEE_DIAG_DIR` writes three chrIV boundary charge curves at boundary 50/91/105, covering **1,101,855 / 7,140,588 / 6,854,524** existing retained allele pairs respectively. Existing `BoundaryCostsStore` already contains composition-level *exact charges*; weight each composition by its realized allele-pair multiplicity and sort the unit-nat Boltzmann log mass `-(exact_cost-min_exact_cost)`, no hypothetical probability from raw pair counts. Save prefix fractions for 50/90/99/99.9% mass, dominant adjacent log-loss gap with pair/mass rank, selected chain's slot-0 boundary cost/rank relative to that gap, an optional *non-certified* profile-feature-count vs negative exact-cost weighted correlation, and the full distinct-composition curve. No pair is dropped, no score/product column changes, no new model constants; the diagnostic uses the already charged draft/cost store and records the selection's actual track (haploid in current chrIV).

**Source falsification of a proposed cheap certificate:** `BoundaryCompositionCharges.floor{,_haploid}` is calculated by MINIMIZING ALL realized `composition_scores` **AFTER** each pooled or novel composition has been scored; it is one constant per boundary, not a per-pair or pre-charge admissible floor. Novel-only `draft.novel_scores` are also computed by the expensive `span.restricted_charge` during draft construction. Neither can certify the unseen pre-enumeration tail or save the 42M-pair work. This is not silently overridden by a profile-length correlation. The current run is observational-only, `run-knee64-chrIV.{log,err,rss,stages,exit,wall,done}` with 64-core guard and curves under `knee-curves-chrIV/`; inspect .done/.exit before reading its results. A production knee needs a newly proved cheap per-pair lower bound, ordered enumeration and a path-level tail certificate before any cut; report this decision to the owner AFTER the curves land, not as approved implementation.

### (b) CHRIV KNEE CURVE DIAGNOSTIC — MASS CONCENTRATION IS REAL; CHARGE-ONLY CUT FAILS ARGMINS
`IMPG_KNEE_DIAG_DIR` chrIV64 diagnostic exits **0 / 777s** (production-form comparator 774s); all selected/reference rescore product columns, both DP totals and 166 legacy boundary stats **BIT-IDENTICAL**. Independent RSS poller peak **40,249,712 KiB** (diagnostic extra curve allocations; comparator production 38,534,328KiB), all below 64GiB guard. Curves `knee-curves-chrIV/boundary-{50,91,105}.json` contain full sorted composition-lumped log-mass curves and every listed prefix/gap/selected-row diagnostic. **Observable is unit-nat normalized exp(-exact BOUNDARY CHARGE) over retained pair multiplicities, NOT a full-chain posterior or certified pre-charge pair mass.** The floor/median/max printed by old boundary stats already prove negative charges; this probe measures the actual curve, not guessed pair uniformity.

| chrIV boundary (degenerate locus) | charged pairs / compositions | 50 / 90 / 99 / 99.9%-mass prefix PAIRS | largest adjacent log-loss gap: prefix pairs, mass before gap | selected full-chain slot-0 pair's CHARGE rank (ties upper) | feature-count vs `-charge` weighted Pearson |
|---|---:|---|---|---:|---:|
| 50 (layer51) | 1,101,855 / 10,006 | 89 / 89 / 96 / 96 | gap 73.20 nats after **1,101,266 pairs**, mass ~1.0 | **26,352** | -.8065 |

The distribution really has knees (91's 10/7,140,588 pair prefix holds ~all CHARGE-ONLY mass). But **the actual SELECTED full-chain argmin uses a boundary-91 pair ranked 7,081,536th by boundary cost** (`selected boundary loss +56.07` versus boundary minimum -241.14 nats): local/window and future path terms overwhelm the isolated seam-charge curve. A charge-only knee at 10 pairs would DISCARD the existing product answer. On 50/105 the largest-gap geometric knee lies almost at the END (1,101,266/1,101,855 and 6,854,523/6,854,524), buying no meaningful pair-work reduction. A cutoff defined instead by 99.9%-mass prefixes would also discard the selected pair at ALL three boundaries. **The owner's argmin-in-dominant-set gate decisively FAILS for the boundary-only observable; no knee pruning was implemented.** The selected pair rank in each JSON is a conservative ties-inclusive upper rank; even tie optimism cannot make 7,081,536 fall within boundary91's 10-pair dominant prefix.

**Certified-bound candidate verdict:** the supposedly existing `BoundaryCompositionCharges.floor{,_haploid}` is calculated from the exact composition charges AFTER enumeration/scoring and is a single constant over all pairs at a boundary; it cannot correlate per pair or pre-screen their mass. `draft.novel_scores` are exact restricted read/record charges already paid during draft build, not cheap floors. The weak measured profile-feature-count correlations on the two 7M-pair boundaries (-.1640/-.1389) are NON-CERTIFIED and inadequate to preserve the chain optimum. Need a NEW lower bound `L(pair) <= exact boundary charge` available BEFORE profile/crossing-record construction, combined with upstream local DP and an admissible downstream suffix, plus a Z/tail-mass certificate, before any geometry-derived prefix can be safe or save time/RSS. No threshold, tail-bound product field, or new model constant was introduced in this diagnostic. The 17k-s genome/100–300s chromosome/GB-class goals therefore remain OPEN; a steep charge-only distribution by itself proves neither safe compression nor cheaper evaluation.

### (c) PROPOSED BOUND DESIGN FOR OWNER APPROVAL — no unapproved knee implementation
Use a pre-charge **record-side per-feature observed upper bound** `O_f(owner)` already derivable from indexed routed/crossing record shares, and an exact lower envelope of the existing loss for ALL nonnegative predicted exposure `s`: `min_{s>=0}[s - O_f log(1+s/beta_f)] = 0` if `O_f<=beta_f`, else `O_f-beta_f-O_f log(O_f/beta_f)`. Sum this conservative envelope over a provable superset of possible seam features for a pair/owner to obtain an admissible seam-charge floor WITHOUT exact MEM profile/record crossing. For restricted charges, `O_f` must cover every record share that `JunctionSpanIndex::restricted_charge` can match (not just observed in the neighboring windows); otherwise the floor is invalid. Add per-state existing local loss and an admissible suffix floor to form a **global-path lower bound**; only exclude a pair when that bound exceeds an actual complete-chain incumbent and when the normalizer/tail upper bound proves the omitted mass budget. The geometry-derived knee would order which candidates to evaluate, but the certificate—not the knee alone—decides whether any later pair can be omitted. Profile-feature prediction and the owner-specific observed superset may themselves cost too much; first instrument their bound tightness and construction time on boundaries 50/91/105 without cutting. If the analytic floor is too loose, do NOT ship the knee; escalate to a separately approved richer pre-charge index. This is a mathematical candidate, NOT an already proved or implemented certificate; the retained selected pair at boundary91 is the indispensable negative control.

### (d) APPROVED ANALYTIC-BOUND PILOT + REAL DP PAIR READS: BOUND CERTIFIED BUT INEFFECTIVE
The owner approved **observation only, no production pruning** at chrIV boundaries 50/91/105, with actual DP pair-address reads. Gate `IMPG_KNEE_BOUND_DIAG_DIR=.../knee-bound-pilot-chrIV`, `run-bound64-chrIV.{log,err,rss,wall,exit,done}` exited **0 / 1,258s**. It is an instrumented run, NOT a new production timing: the 19.218 BILLION dynamic read-counter operations at boundary50 add substantial overhead. Externally polled peak **46,540,922,880 B** (45,450,120 KiB), versus clean production's 39,459,151,872 B; guard held. Every rescore product field (excluding timing), BOTH diploid/haploid DP totals, and all 166 legacy boundary stats are BIT-IDENTICAL to the final scoped width-16 production gate. One erroneously started **stale-binary** pilot was terminated with exit143 before this gate, rebuilt with `cargo build --release --example panel_route_mem_routed`, and rerun cleanly with fresh artifacts; it is excluded from ALL findings.

The tested mathematical bound is **valid only for restricted novel charges AFTER exact predicted feature keys are already built**: for each such feature count EVERY read-chain occurrence of EVERY placed record in the junction index (`O_f`); every eligible crossing read/record is a subset of these occurrences and its routed owner share is <=1. For `beta=model.background>0`, q≥0 implies `s=q*histogram*depth/denominator≥0`, so restricted loss `s-o*log(1+s/beta) ≥ min_{s≥0}[s-O_f*log(1+s/beta)]`, which is `0` if `O_f≤beta` and `O_f-beta-O_f*log(O_f/beta)` otherwise. Summing over the ACTUAL predicted feature keys is an admissible post-profile lower bound. Its construction does NOT need `restricted_charge` crossing queries, but the existing draft has already executed them; to put it before that work requires a separate cheaply constructed SUPerset of possible predicted seam keys (NOT present/proved). For co-occurring pooled compositions (including the novel-riding-pooled cases), background multiplicities and observation semantics differ; this pilot does **not** certify their floors. Across all 65,943 tested novel compositions in these three boundaries, the lower envelope had **0 observed violations** (threshold `1e-7`); structural subset proof above does not rely on this numerical check.

No practically useful pre-charge block floors are certified, and the 42M-pair matrix cannot yet be lazily charged by this bound.

## HISTORICAL, SUPERSEDED FOUR-WAY GLOBAL-MOLECULE ASSESSMENT (not the per-locus genotype verdict)
**SUPERSESSION #1: the former `0.5169026601` score is invalid as a diploid distance.** That scorer assigned every *block* of ONE inferred molecule to whichever of the TWO truth molecules matched best; it credited the same slot with **10,223,561** S288C correct bp AND **1,799,189** SK1 correct bp. One physical molecule cannot simultaneously supply both haplotypes. Preserve the old `distance-*.json` in `distance-superseded-anytruth/` (18 JSON files) solely as superseded evidence. The canonical `distance-genome.json` and all 17 `distance-{smoke2|fleet}-<component>.json` were replaced by the corrected assessor; original product routes, source FASTA, production walls and reference scores were NOT rerun or changed.

**The four-way rule actually scored:** in each component infer slots `I1`,`I2` (I2 is EMPTY for every selected product), truths `T1=S288C`, `T2=SK1`. Evaluate BOTH global permutations `I1→T1, I2→T2` AND `I1→T2, I2→T1`; select the lower total distance, deterministic original-order tie break. Every predicted window/block of a given slot is aligned ONLY to its globally assigned truth for distance; reverse alignments/fragmented blocks allowed. Credit each assigned truth coordinate once across the window-indexed output (once-per-material union), charge all uncovered assigned truth bases (including ALL bases assigned to an EMPTY slot) plus query insertions and unaligned query bp. Denominator is the FULL sum of the two actual truth path lengths, **24,390,865 bp**. Independently map the same query against BOTH truths **only for ancestry diagnostics**. Blocks best aligning to the OTHER truth have source-named query/truth interval extents, strand, edits and correct-bp counts in each component JSON, but NEVER cover that other molecule and NEVER yield an extra error credit. Their disagreement/omission relative to the globally ASSIGNED truth is charged in the fixed-configuration distance, not separately charged again as a crossover penalty; a crossover is an ANCESTRY/STRUCTURE label, NOT a fabricated two-slot phase-switch call or an assertion of a de novo mutation. Local mapper candidates are not a proof of complete repeat-placement search; the identical-rDNA self alignment has its existing exact identity certificate. The per-window route is not a standalone assembled molecule.

**CORRECTED DIPLOID VERDICT (17/17 existing saved routes): `12,785,205 / 24,390,865 = 0.5241800567548547` two-copy sequence distance.** Every component chose **I1→S288C, empty I2→SK1**; the alternative I1→SK1 / empty I2→S288C totals **13,861,277 / 24,390,865 = 0.568297885294351** (sum of the 17 alternative component configurations). The winning slot covers **11,936,936 S288C** truth bp, **11,904,238** of them matching: **99.726077%** concordance on its *assigned* covered truth. SK1 has **0 covered / 12,147,923 truth bp** because its assigned inferred slot is empty: its per-assigned-truth covered-bp concordance is **UNDEFINED (0/0)**, not zero sequence identity. S288C has **306,006** uncovered truth bp; query insertions **71,138**, unaligned query bp **227,440**; `24,390,865 - 11,904,238 + 71,138 + 227,440 = 12,785,205`. The previous `0.5169026601` understated errors by **177,502 bp** (0.0072774 distance units) despite its 1.8M SK1 credit, because conserved SK1-like blocks also match the assigned S288C sequence at many bases. **Do not infer that the false cross-truth credit should be subtracted wholesale or that the corrected 0.524 is a functional diploid product: the second molecule is completely missing.**

## FINAL OWNER-CONFIRMED DIPLOID ASSESSMENT: INJECTIVE LOCAL GENOTYPE PAIRS ON ATTESTED HOMOLOG WINDOWS
**Third supersession; this section replaces BOTH 0.5169026601 (a single call block-wise credited against both truths) and 0.5241800568 (whole-chromosome slot↔truth assignment).** Both evaluated at the wrong unit: the inference outputs a separate **unordered local genotype pair** at each locus, not a physically assembled global two-molecule FASTA. New assessment-only source `genome/instrumented/score-diploid-genotype.py`, serial saved-route runner `run-diploid-genotype.sh`, and source-named tests compute the owner's primitive:

For each locus with a **full-length, primary, unique, positive-mapq alignment** of the S288C axis window onto SK1 (the SAME preexisting mappy orthology/correspondence gate as `score-diploid.py`), let `T1` be the S288C local allele and `T2` the oriented SK1 local homolog's **full attested span**. Spell the saved two per-locus called rows as `C1,C2`; for this fleet `C2=EMPTY` everywhere. Local edit costs use edlib **global NW distance** on the full local allele strings; `d(EMPTY,T)=len(T)`. The locus's **injective** two-copy error is `min[d(C1,T1)+d(C2,T2), d(C1,T2)+d(C2,T1)]`. Do not greedily match both calls to one truth. Sum errors and the two local truth allele lengths over ALL **attested** loci for a genome-weighted distance. The non-attested SK1 windows have NO local ortholog sequence authorized by this correspondence rule: no fabricated zero-length `T2`, no score/no implied accuracy; count them as separate brackets with their S288C axis bp, retaining locus IDs and null assignment. Truth self and swapped-self local matches are zero, including inversion-oriented SK1 alleles.

**DIPLOID VERDICT ON THE ASSESSABLE 753/1,307 LOCI: `7,693,342 / 15,001,948 = 0.5128228680701999` per-locus two-truth genotype distance.** Of the **7,693,342** errors, **7,489,051** are the full lengths of attested local truth alleles assigned to the EMPTY inferred second slot; the occupied call contributes **204,291** edlib disagreement bp. The other **554/1,307** loci (S288C axis **4,751,852** bp) are **BRACKETED: this yardstick CANNOT assess their SK1 allele**. The 15,001,948-bp denominator is the **SUM OF BOTH LOCAL TRUTH ALLELE LENGTHS AT ATTESTED LOCI**, not the former whole-physical-truth 24,390,865-bp denominator. The result MUST NOT be presented as a complete 1,307-locus two-copy genome distance or as one molecule covering two homologs. Original production's all-single-slot result remains a DIPLOID PRODUCT FAILURE: one real row plus an empty row at each of 1,307 windows, one dosage class of 2 versus truth S288C 1.2/SK1 0.8.

**Per-locus assignment = ancestry/crossover structure, not two-haplotype phase quality.** Every source-named `genotype-distance-{smoke2|fleet}-<component>.json` includes 1 row per locus, the oriented SK1 ortholog interval when assessable, both DIRECT and SWAPPED edit totals, chosen slot↔truth assignment, whether the choice ties, the actual empty-slot full-length charge, and a same-length `assignment_track` (`null` at bracketed loci). 627 assessable loci assign the occupied call to S288C, 120 to SK1; 6 exact-cost ties use deterministic direct representation but have **unidentifiable ancestry**. Across **502 strictly adjacent, assessable, non-tied boundaries**, the assignment changes **126** times; unknown gaps/ties are NOT bridged and no two-slot phase-switch rate is claimed. The 805/1,307 truth-pair-*candidate-domain* gaps are a DIFFERENT, larger structural frame metric than the 554 sequence-orthology brackets: chrIV has 98/167 locally assessable SK1 alleles despite 0/167 exact two-truth pair expressibility in the candidate domain. Saved Item-2 `[truth-pieces]` logs have no copy1 material at 801/1,307 loci, including **413 of the 753 alignment-attested SK1 homolog windows**; treating that absence as an empty physical SK1 allele could silently erase their missing-homolog error. This is NOT the same count as 805 candidate-domain pair gaps; the other 4 have copy1 pieces but no expressible pair. Detected and forbidden before this scorer was run. The owner expressly ruled out manufacturing a new complete SK1 projection across inversions/fragmentations.

**Currency/limitations:** the assessment compares FULL local called material to FULL attested local truth material by NW; the two alleles are used without replacement at EACH locus. Summation follows the owner's per-window genotype product; it does not turn overlapping windows into a deduplicated physical assembly and does not alter the product m1 once-per-material/refund/reference columns. A local SK1 ortholog absent under the approved full-primary-unique alignment rule remains bracketed, not a mutation, success or zero-length truth. Haplotype dosage is reported separately. Historic haploid mosaic `acc_H=.9611032941432163` is from a DIFFERENT sample; neither it nor the two superseded physical-distance values share this attested local denominator. Continue Item 3 and the partition-native spine to address two-molecule inference; they are designed-next, not shipped.

### Quarantined cosine pilot: chrI and chrVI (PARTIAL, not the 502-locus verdict)
Read-only diagnostic from saved reads/index/panel reran both stress components serially under the 64GiB guard and preserved selected DP route/three references. Arm A's observed vector is read-record placement coverage on physical panel-path material: each record contributes its existing routed equal share `multiplicity / touched_partition_count` once per covered base after deduplicating its overlapping MEM-subwalk spans; expected per candidate segment is +15 coverage per real copy. Disjoint bins split at all material/placement endpoints; cosine sums bp-weighted dot/norm. Arm B compares the SAME candidates' expected oracle MEM FeatureKey-count profiles (15× per copy; all expected-only features included in their norms) against the existing `window_obs` mass. The candidate set is truth pair + selected pair + eight best unrestricted current-objective class pairs + eight best PORT-VIABLE current-objective class pairs, with identical physical pair identities deduplicated; **ranks are RESTRICTED to this explicitly sampled set, not all diplotypes**. The first narrow pilot omitted unrestricted pairs; its 17/17 rank1 finding is SUPERSEDED by this corrected comparison.

On chrI's **17/17** expressible loci, arm A ranks truth **#1 including ties 16/17 (11 unique)** and <=#2 17/17; arm B #1 16/17 (10 unique), <=#2 16/17 (one #4). chrVI's **2/2**: arm A #1 2/2 (one unique); arm B #1 2/2 (one unique). Thus combined stress arm A #1 **18/19**, B #1 **18/19** *among sampled alternatives*; old production exact selected pair was **0/19**. These tests support trying cosine but show NO unique arm-A-versus-arm-B explanation at these stress loci. They DO NOT license a fleet cosine success claim or objective replacement before the full 502-locus comparison.

**Exactly what was compared, without smuggling a product change:** source `examples/panel_route_spine/cosine_probe.rs` is env-gated by `IMPG_COSINE_DIAG_OUTPUT` and writes separate JSONL after the unchanged diploid DP; no sweep/DP/posterior/reference output reads its result. The existing routing pass supplies each record's physical MEM-subwalk placements and established equal share `multiplicity/touched_partition_count`. ARM A unions a record's overlapping placement spans on each panel path, contributing the equal share **once per covered base per record**, not once per overlapping subwalk; it intersects with the union of the candidate alleles' actual source intervals and emits disjoint `(path,lo,hi,coverage)` bins, including zero-coverage material. A candidate diplotype expects +15 per occupied copy at each covered physical base, and cosine dot/norm are bp-width weighted. ARM B uses the SAME physical allele pairs' MEM-subwalk oracle profiles multiplied by depth15, the observed `window_obs` FeatureKey shares, and includes predicted-only keys in the expected norm. Neither arm imports the old omission-charge likelihood, its multiplicity backgrounds or its post-hoc reference truth into the observed vector. Truth is assessment-side only. Orientations that spell the same physical source material can tie; this diagnostic is not an ancestry/phasing test. An observed record placing on multiple panel paths may contribute its routed equal share at each path, matching the routing share convention; it is not a uniquely mapped read.

**Restriction/equivalence disclosure:** for each expressible locus the comparison includes the exact truth pair, actual selected ordered pair, the eight best *unrestricted* finite class pairs by the old objective (even if port-nonviable), and the eight best *port-viable* finite class pairs; each class is represented by its first member or first viable member, with duplicate physical allele-index pairs collapsed. There are **9–18 distinct tested physical pairs/locus**; the many non-materialized other diplotypes were NOT ranked. Thus `447/498` is **restricted-comparison rank1**, not proof of a full-domain cosine argmax, not a 447/498 exact genotype call, and not license to delete read-accounting or reference columns. Arm-A full 498-wrong rank histogram: ranks `1:447, 2:30, 3:9, 4:7, 5:3, 7:1, 8:1`; arm B `1:422, 2:39, 3:22, 4:4, 5:1, 6:2, 7:5, 9:2, 10:1`. Of all 502, arm A rank1 incl ties **451** (unique **378**), B **426** (unique **351**). Restriction means any future all-pair production objective needs an exhaustive or certified candidate-universe gate before claiming accuracy.

**What the experiment supports and does NOT support:** the actual implementation compared predicted MEM subwalk/window-mass profiles to routed mass using background-relative Poisson and a dominant in-window omission charge. `docs/syng-gmem-bwt/partition-local-diplotype-inference.md` deliberately borrowed COSIGT's *pair enumeration* but explicitly substituted Poisson for cosine; labeling that older likelihood "COSIGT-pattern local scoring" was incorrect. The owner's coverage-vector comparison is a measured *better ranking on this restricted diagnostic*: A gives **25 more rank1 rescues** than B on 498 wrong-selected loci, though B itself recovers **422/498**. Consequently **the likelihood/comparison form is directly implicated**, but the claim that the *pooled observed vector alone* causes all failure is NOT supported: cosine even in that feature space often ranks truth at/near top. Likewise, A still fails rank1 at **51/498** sampled comparisons and ties at many loci; the proven external COSIGT result cannot simply be transferred as complete-genome success here.

**Independent structural blocker to an objective-only fix:** saved v2 `genotype_calls.loci[].truth.pair_viable` reports only **5/502** exact truth pairs port-viable (4 chrII, 1 chrX); chrI **0/17**, chrVI **0/2**. This matches the diagnostic's independently reconstructed `truth_alleles_port_viable` and `truth_classes_port_viable` totals. **497/502** expressible truth pairs cannot be selected through the current unchanged local viable-pair gate, even if a new cosine score ranks them first. This is a separate constraint from the 805/1307 unexpressible candidate-domain gaps and the 554 alignment-assessment brackets. To build a product correction, an owner-reviewed domain/port-viability remedy AND a tested cosine-comparison selection objective are needed; neither was approved for implementation in this measurement-only turn. Item 3 as *new likelihood* is superseded by the owner's instruction to test the proven comparison first; graph-native domain/viability work and per-record refinements remain separate, unresolved tasks.

**Owner ruling on CONDITIONAL next design (not a production rewrite):** the 447/498 restricted physical-coverage rank1 count is sufficient to **DESIGN**, not to deploy, a COSIGT-style coverage-vector local selection. The owner requires three sequential review gates, documented in `docs/syng-gmem-bwt/cosine-coverage-objective.md`: (1) factorized coverage-space cosine from per-row observed dot `d_i`, norm `n_i` and row-pair overlap `g_ij`, scoring all pairs in O(rows²) simple arithmetic with streamed ranks/ties; (2) correct the separately measured port-viability/admission defect only after explaining its path topology and physical route legality; (3) run an EXHAUSTIVE full-domain cosine ranking on all 502 expressible loci (not the 9–18-pair pilot), name high-cosine wrong competitors, confirm full-rank truth/tie counts before any selection rewrite. The old FeatureKey profile-class equality cannot collapse physically different coverage rows without exact vector equivalence. Product scores, reference rows, once-per-material/junction accounting and the truth assessment mask remain preserved and separate.

**Port-viability rule's measured cause:** `spine_viability` intersects full-component forward and backward reachability through `successors_ref` from exact `port_word_seams`, plus local internal-span feasibility. Across the 502 expressible truth pairs, **S288C copy0 passes 502/502; SK1 copy1 passes 5/502**. Of **497** SK1 failures, **48** lack an immediate incoming or outgoing port-word link, while **449 have BOTH immediate links but cannot connect to both chromosome ends through that port-word graph** (dead-end propagation). Independently parsed all 502 attested truth interval lists: **zero** self-overlapping same-source segments in either truth copy, so local `spans_feasible_local` is not this failure. This is the measured distinction between a real locally genotypable SK1 allele and a broken *global reference-window port reachability* filter; it is not evidence that every arbitrary disconnected panel row should be admitted. The design proposes scoring locally valid material first and enforcing physical route continuity/endpoint/seam legality in a separately proved phase layer. No viability predicate, likelihood or DP was changed by the diagnostic.

Independent 17/17 checks confirmed each diagnostic locus's old/new truth-domain mask matches `genotype-distance-balanced-*.json`, 502 expressible and exactly four selected truth pairs; all 17 diagnostic selected route-pair tracks and `truth/native2/haploid_truth` reference labels match the frozen balanced production results. The authoritative balanced `run-balanced-*` product logs, `genotype-distance-balanced-*` scorer JSONs, saved reads/index and reference columns were neither overwritten nor rescored for this diagnostic. **SIDE EFFECT DISCLOSED:** the existing component runner used the shared data-dir cwd, so the full diagnostic reruns REFRESHED derived relative `spine-rescore/` scratch sidecars under that directory (no pre-diagnostic copy exists). Repository clean/no staged files; nothing pushed to PR #243. Next owner decision gates: review the objective/port-admission design, certify exhaustive all-domain cosine rank (including ties/competitors) via row-factorized pair arithmetic, then separately approve any production selection rewrite and physical-route viability change.

**Construction, validity and falsification:** `IMPG_COSINE_DOMAIN_PILOT` filters Stage-1 rows to original `single:` source material or `stitch:` traversals with strictly adjacent same-source/same-orientation segments. Cross-source `split:`, port-cut and `cooc-partial` constructs remain in their historic lanes but are excluded from this diploid pure-route pilot. On chrMT/chrI **no such constructed Stage-1 row was present**, so filtering removed **zero** rows; the *hypothesized* ~34-path row contraction was NOT measured. Exactly identical physical path-coordinate interval-union vectors (including orientation-alias and copy duplicates) coalesce for cosine scoring, but identities remain inspectable, and distinct material is not merged by the old pooled FeatureKey profile-class signature. Every admissible unordered vector pair is scored by `(<x,e_i>+<x,e_j>)/sqrt(||x||²(||e_i||²+||e_j||²+2<e_i,e_j>))`, with bp-overlap row dot/norm precomputed and every record's overlapping placement spans unioned once before m/t share addition. No Poisson/omission scores, reference truth, oracle-projected copy, or source-family prior enters the observed vector or pair score; attested truth enters only rank assessment. A `1e-12` score-equivalence convention from the earlier diagnostic counts near ties, not a genotype-selection threshold. The fixed observation universe grows when all physically present paths enter; this changes EVERY candidate's common norm by the SAME factor per locus and cannot reverse candidate rank. The pilot reported only Stage-1 local comparisons, not whole-molecule DP outcomes. The old `4/502` product genotype verdict and old reference columns remain the saved baseline, not an after-change scoreboard.

**Receipt gates:** `run-cosine-exhaustive-pilot-{chrMT,chrI}.{done,exit=0,wall,rss,stages}` and driver done/exit markers; serial runner `--spine-sweep-only`, given balanced depth15 per homolog, given two-real diploid prior and original 64GiB guard. Pilot-specific cwd uses `cosine-diagnostic-scratch/{component}`; shared old rescore scratch is not touched by these new sweep-only pilots. `cargo test --release --example panel_route_mem_routed cosine_` passed **4/4**, including direct-base-vector vs row-factorized pair score, exact material-vector duplicate coalescing, and within-record coverage-span union; release example built. `python3 -B genome/instrumented/check-cosine-exhaustive-receipt.py` independently reconciled old/new truth-presence mask, 8+17 truth survivors, pair-score file's every better-cosine record and its rank counts; `python3 -B genome/instrumented/test-score-cosine-probe.py` 3/3; runner `bash -n` and diff checks passed. Pilot walls are NOT full production walls and may not be compared as a speedup. No claim to the 100–300s/chromosome / near-100MB targets.

**Graph-space pivot, subject to OWNER REVIEW BEFORE implementation:** represent both observed read-placement coverage and per-allele expected coverage on SHARED graph segment/offset coordinates, so two conserved source placements occupy the SAME physical segment features and divergent alleles occupy their own graph segments. The same factorized row arithmetic applies; preserve node-visit copy multiplicity, repeated paths, orientation, read-share conservation and independent path-spelling identity. A crucial prerequisite is missing: `panel_routes::Graph` stores source lanes and ports, not a shared-node coordinate map; `src/commands/genotype.rs::NormalizedGraph` reads actual GFA segment/path steps, but the located historical GFA artifacts only show selected partition examples, not a verified complete 17-component binding to these source routes. There is NO honest graph-space numerical rank yet. The revised owner-review design is `docs/syng-gmem-bwt/cosine-coverage-objective.md`. Separately, global port reachability still rejects 497/502 already present SK1 truth rows; no viability change is justified by the failed row-space objective, and the 805 candidate-domain gaps remain. **Stop here** for owner review of graph construction, typed read-to-node projection and graph-space score validation before any further production selection/rerun. No PR #243 push.

**Commit/algorithm qualification:** diagnostic code/independent receipt verifier committed `3a34bb5`; row-space refutation and graph-space revised design committed `6dfc100`. The pilot precomputes the observed dot and expected norm for every exactly collapsed material vector. It computes `g_ij` by walking sorted disjoint material intervals **for each tested pair**, avoiding a dense pair table; on the overwhelmingly single-interval rows this is constant work per pair, but arbitrary multi-segment rows incur intersection work and are NOT a certified production `O(rows²)` arithmetic implementation. The graph-space design requires sparse shared-segment pair-overlap precomputation to meet that stricter objective cost bound before any future production implementation. Final `git status --porcelain=v1` empty; no files staged; no PR push.

## 2026-10-01 SESSION OPEN — GRAPH-SPACE CONDENSATION + NODES/EDGES FULL-DOMAIN GATE (chrMT/chrI)

Owner go-ahead: build the graph-space evidence condensation (observed = routed share mass on graph segments PLUS edge-layer adjacency usage from span-index crossing records; expected = candidate diplotype's two paths' node+edge usage at depth 15; factorized cosine over combined node+edge space), then EXHAUSTIVE full-domain ranking on chrMT and chrI over ALL candidates, naming near-twin competitors and truth's whole-domain rank, with nodes-only vs nodes+edges as separate measured numbers. Stage scope is the FIRST CHECKPOINT ONLY: this build + gate, committed with measured numbers. No 502-locus proof, no selection swap, no PR push. Writing begins; incremental updates follow.

**Increment 1 — graph-space build design (before implementation).** The panel graph used is the loaded syng GBWT itself (`SyngIndex`): its nodes are the signed syncmer k-mer segments (1-based ids, sign = strand; ~10.0M vertices for the 235-strain panel), shared across paths wherever sequence is conserved — `panel.walk_path_range(path, lo, hi)` spells (node, bp) steps along any panel path, and node identity is frame-independent (the territory code documents this). This supplies the previously missing shared-node coordinate map without inventing an alignment: conserved placements collapse to shared node ids by the panel's own interning; diverged k-mers are distinct nodes. Observed (node layer): per locus, each contributing routed record's equal share `m/t` votes once per graph node whose k-mer window is fully contained in the union of the record's routed placement spans (all features, both frames, merged per path; per-record per-node dedup across paths — the read votes a shared node once however many conserved paths it places on; the `physical_material_row` routing convention stays). Observed (edge layer): the adjacencies the placed records span — consecutive covered steps along the placement path form edge votes `(abs(node_t), abs(node_{t+1}))` ordered by bp, same per-record once-per-edge dedup and share mass; a junction adjacency that no single record spans would need the span index's chain-pair crossing machinery, but the pure-route domain cannot contain such junctions (admissible rows are `single:` or strictly adjacent same-source `stitch:`, so every spelled adjacency is same-path continuous and abundantly spanned by single records) — the measured multi-segment/stitch row counts are reported per locus so the edge layer's chain channel is an audited zero, not an assumption. Expected: each candidate row's node/edge usage = its merged per-path intervals' fully-contained steps and their consecutive pairs, depth 15 per covered copy with repeat-visit multiplicity preserved. Cosine factorization carries over per row: `d_i` (node dot + edge dot), `n_i` (node norm + edge norm), `g_ij` (node overlap + edge overlap) — two separate score arms over the same coalesced row universe (coalesced on EXACT node-multiset + edge-multiset equality): (A) nodes-only, (B) nodes+edges. The edge layer's contribution is reported as separate measured numbers (per-locus rank deltas between arms, edge masses, competitor counts). Truth assessment-side only via the existing exact-segment truth index; 1e-12 tie convention; fail-closed zero norms; exhaustive ALL-pairs enumeration per locus over the entire domain; no thresholds, no constants. Gate env: `IMPG_COSINE_GRAPH_OUTPUT` (requires `IMPG_COSINE_DOMAIN_PILOT`), dump beside the row-space pilot in `cosine_probe.rs`, receipts `run-cosine-graph-pilot-{chrMT,chrI}.*`, serial, stdbuf, 64GiB guard, sweep-only, pilot cwd redirect. Scope: chrMT + chrI first checkpoint only; no 502 run, no selection/viability change, no PR push.

**Increment 2 — implementation state.** `examples/panel_route_spine/cosine_probe.rs` now carries `dump_graph_exhaustive` (env `IMPG_COSINE_GRAPH_OUTPUT`, requires the pure-route `IMPG_COSINE_DOMAIN_PILOT` gate, hooked in `mod.rs` beside the row-space pilot with `rss_probe`): per locus it gathers every candidate row's merged per-path material and every contributing routed record's merged placement spans (all features, both frames), does ONE bounding-range `panel.walk_path_range` walk per path, extracts window-contained syncmer nodes and their consecutive-step adjacency edges per interval, builds the candidate-usage universe, accumulates per-record once-per-node/edge share votes, coalesces rows on exact (node-multiset, edge-multiset) equality, and scores ALL unordered graph-row pairs in both arms (nodes-only; nodes+edges) with the factored arithmetic (`d_i`/`n_i`/`g_ij`, 1e-12 tie convention, fail-closed zero norms). Census streams per-locus domains, identity kinds, the path-continuity audit (`multi_segment_rows`, `disjoint_material_rows`), universes, masses, eligible pairs per arm, truth presence/rank/ties per arm, best per arm, competitor-entry counts; the competitors file names every pair above truth in either arm with identities, node/edge counts and both arm scores. Rust tests: 9/9 `cosine_` pass (incl. new graph factorization-vs-direct-dense pair score with repeat-visit multiplicity and a same-row double-copy pair, window containment, multiset overlap, packed-edge ordering, exact-equality coalescing, row bp continuity). chrMT pilot launched (serial, sweep-only, pilot cwd redirect, stdbuf, RSS poller + 64GiB guard).

**Increment 3 — chrMT first checkpoint measured (serial run, exit 0, wall 49s, peak external RSS 13,805,280 kB, internal guard clean).** Graph-space condensation over the FULL chrMT domain (2,711 physical rows, all `single:`, `multi_segment_rows=0` and `disjoint_material_rows=0` at every locus — the chain-junction channel is a MEASURED empty set, not an assumption) coalesces to 59-157 graph rows per locus (near-twin collapse). EXHAUSTIVE all-pairs, both arms, over all 14 loci: **nodes-only truth rank1 2/8, rank<=2 3/8** (ranks by locus: 1,1,1,2,3,3,5,94); **nodes+edges rank1 2/8, rank<=2 3/8** (ranks: 1,1,1,2,3,3,5,15). Versus the row-space refutation's 1/8 rank1 and worst rank 562, the shared-node space rescues one locus to rank1 and collapses the deep failures: locus2 562->1, locus3 56->3, locus6 91->14(nodes)/11(edges), locus8 22->3, locus9 13->5, locus10 10->2. The EDGE LAYER'S CONTRIBUTION IS A SEPARATE NUMBER: it changes truth rank at 2/8 loci (locus3 94->15, locus6 14->11), never worsens one; 115 distinct higher-scoring pair entries streamed with identities (row-space: 752). Edge observed mass 9,093.7 vs node mass 18,935.1. chrI launched serially.

**Against the row-space refutation, per locus:** chrMT improves everywhere (L2 562→1, L3 56→3, L6 91→14, L8 22→3, L9 13→5, L10 10→2). chrI is MIXED, not a uniform rescue: gains L6 (29→1), L4 (66→2), L5 (22→6), L3 (36→14), L19 (316→107), L2 (135→129); LOSES row-space's only chrI rank1 (L8: 1→1,275) and worsens L7 (142→1,695), L10 (5→2,833), L12 (55→1,111), L14 (16→1,395), L15 (1,362→9,025); L13 stays deep (3,201→3,437).

**The edge layer's contribution as separate numbers:** observed edge mass 9,093.7 (chrMT) / 40,653.7 (chrI) share units vs node mass 18,935.1 / 109,949.1; it improves truth rank at 2/8 chrMT loci (L3 94→15, L6 14→11) and 14/17 chrI loci (L3 14→2, L19 107→15, L13 3,437→2,297, L15 9,025→6,357, …), worsens exactly one (chrI L8 by 1), and changes NO rank1 count. Higher-scoring distinct pairs above truth: nodes arm 115 (chrMT) / 23,286 (chrI); combined arm 33 / 18,463; every one named with identities, node/edge counts and both arm scores in `cosine-graph-exhaustive-{chrMT,chrI}.jsonl.competitors.jsonl`. chrI locus2's top competitor is STILL the row-space-exposed strain interval `5397:[23841,29584)` (now paired with `2387:[18027,23247)`, combined 0.50954 vs truth 0.50419); chrMT locus3's top: `9282:[17294,27497)` + `6244:[45216,52519)` (0.47193 vs 0.42164).

**Construction and validity:** the graph is the loaded panel GBWT itself — nodes are the syng syncmer segments (signed 1-based ids, sign = storage strand, identity frame-independent, interned shared across all 235 strain paths), edges are consecutive-covered-node adjacencies along placement paths — the previously missing shared-node map is the panel's own interning, verified by the existing territory/walk machinery, not an invented alignment. Coverage counts a shared segment/adjacency iff the k-mer window is fully contained (both observed and expected sides, symmetric); observed is each contributing routed record's equal share m/t once per node and once per spanned adjacency (per-record dedup across conserved placements = the near-twin collapse: chrMT 2,711→1,126, chrI 9,213→3,679 rows, 59-60% coalescing); expected is depth 15 per covered copy with repeat-visit multiplicity; rows coalesce ONLY on exact node+edge multiset equality; the universe is the candidates' own usage (mass outside dropped, exactly the row-space convention); fail-closed zero norms; no thresholds/constants; truth enters only rank assessment via the exact-segment index. The span-index chain-junction channel is a MEASURED empty set in this pure-route domain: every locus reports `multi_segment_rows=0`, `disjoint_material_rows=0`, all rows `single:` (each spelled adjacency is same-path continuous and single-record-spanned); the audit counters are in the per-locus census, so the emptiness is re-checked every run, not assumed.

**Receipts:** `run-cosine-graph-pilot-{chrMT,chrI}.{done,exit=0,wall=48s/70s,rss,stages}` via the unchanged `run-balanced-diploid-component.sh` with `IMPG_COSINE_DOMAIN_PILOT=1 IMPG_COSINE_GRAPH_OUTPUT=…` (serial, sweep-only, pilot cwd redirect under `cosine-diagnostic-scratch/`, stdbuf line-buffered, 64GiB internal guard clean: internal peaks 13,780 MiB / 15,396 MiB at the graph dump stage; final-run external poller peaks 13,830,804 kB / 15,765,552 kB). **Disclosure:** the `.rss`/`.stages` poller sidecars for chrI (and chrMT's first 23:17 attempt) accumulated samples from earlier same-pilot attempts (an initial full chrI run with the pre-`competitor_entries` binary, then a rerun killed by a 60s shell timeout mid-genome-background before its dump completed); the committed receipts are from the FINAL serial runs and the quoted peaks are final-run-only; nothing else differs between attempts (deterministic binary apart from the added census counter). `check-cosine-graph-receipt.py` independently reconciles receipt markers, locus alignment with the balanced Stage-1 census AND the row-space pilot, identical physical domains/truth masks, both arms' exhaustive pair bounds, every competitor's arm flags/scores/truth references and totals (115/33, 23,286/18,463), the path-continuity audit zeros, and the arm-delta counts (2 and 15 changed loci); `cargo test --release --example panel_route_mem_routed cosine_` **9/9** (graph factorization vs direct dense vectors with repeat multiplicity and same-row double-copy pairs, window containment, multiset overlap, packed-edge ordering, exact-equality coalescing, plus the prior row-space tests); `python3 -B genome/instrumented/test-score-cosine-probe.py` 3/3; runner `bash -n`; `git diff --check`/`--cached --check` clean. Commit **245ff08** (code + checker + measured doc section); final `git status --porcelain` empty; nothing staged; **no PR #243 push**.

**What this checkpoint supports and does NOT:** the shared-node condensation is a measured strict improvement on chrMT (2/8 vs 1/8 incl ties; deep failures collapsed) and a lateral move on chrI (1/17 vs 1/17, different loci rescued/lost), with the edge layer a consistent small positive that never rescues a rank1 alone. ## 2026-10-01 QUAL EMISSION STAGE COMPLETE — MATERIAL-CLASS CALLED SETS + PHRED QUAL, EXHAUSTIVE chrMT/chrI

**Supervisor decision received mid-stage (the ruling's formula and its worked example were mathematically inconsistent at tied called sets; escalated, not silently decided):** the owner's refined product semantics are MATERIAL-CLASS level — candidates that are the same effective material are not alternatives ("low quality is not bad here because maybe the other alternatives are the SAME effectively"), and member multiplicity must not inflate confidence. Concretely: (1) candidates with bit-identical node+edge usage signatures coalesce into one material class — already true in the stage-1 condensation at ROW level, so a candidate class is an unordered pair of coalesced graph rows and the class weight is the class's OWN similarity value, never the member-summed one; (2) share = s_win / (sum of s_c over DISTINCT material classes); (3) QUAL = -10*log10(1 - share) per locus, and a k-way tie means k DISTINCT classes at the max, with the bound Q = -10*log10(1-1/k) for an all-mass k-class tie (the B reading, at class level); (4) the called set is the distinct classes at the bit-identical maximum, emitted as class signatures, not member route identities. Exact-tie convention stated: identical f64 score values; every score is finite, non-NaN, non-negative and -0.0 cannot arise (non-negative dots over positive norms), so IEEE-754 equality IS bit identity; NO epsilon constant enters the product call. The owner's example formula is a THEOREM at class level: for a k-way tie at s, the k classes alone contribute k*s to the denominator, so share <= 1/k and Q <= -10*log10(1-1/k), equality iff the tie holds all the domain's similarity mass.

**Implementation:** `examples/panel_route_spine/cosine_probe.rs::dump_graph_exhaustive` now accumulates, per locus on the nodes+edges arm (the stage-1 machinery), the total similarity over every distinct class (deterministic enumeration order), collects the called set at the bit-identical maximum, and emits per locus: `qual_similarity_total`, `qual_best_similarity`, `qual_called_class_count`, `qual_called_classes` (row indices, per-row member counts, physical-pair multiplicity, node/edge counts, usage signature hashes, usage distance and DIFFERING OBSERVED MASS versus the first called class, is_truth_class), `qual_called_physical_pairs`, `qual_truth_in_called_set`, `qual_share`, `qual_unbounded`, `qual` (null when unbounded or massless — never clamped), plus a new `.ties.jsonl` stream: every class within the stage-1 1e-12 window of the truth class (truth class included) with ULP delta, bit-exactness flag, node/edge usage distance AND differing observed mass versus the truth class, shared-row count, member counts. `genome/instrumented/check-cosine-graph-receipt.py` extended: re-derives share (exact IEEE division), Phred QUAL, physical-pair multiplicities, truth-in-called-set (twice: by class membership and by bit-equality of the truth score with the max — equivalent because the called set is every class at the bit-identical max), asserts the k-way bound fail-closed, reconciles the ties stream against the stage-1 tied counter, and verifies the re-run reproduces every stage-1 integer/mask/rank field exactly with a DOCUMENTED cross-run tolerance on the six HashMap-ordered float fields (the observed-norm sums and the cosines derived from them vary a few f64 ULPs across processes — measured max 4e-16 relative, changing no rank, tie or count; the tolerance is receipt reconciliation, not a product constant). Four new Rust tests; scoring machinery unmodified.

Truth-in-called-set: **chrMT 2/8, chrI 1/17** — exactly the three corrected unique rank-1 loci.

**QUAL semantics validation (measured):** (1) Exact ties land at the derived bound: the clean all-mass k-way tie lands bit-identically at Q = -10*log10(1-1/k) (unit-proven for k=2,3,5,9; any competitor mass strictly lowers share, unit-proven and checker-asserted). Measured k=2 called-set ties: chrMT locus7 Q 0.00060 and chrI locus14 Q 0.00033 versus the all-mass k=2 bound 3.0103 — far below BECAUSE the ties hold only 1.4e-4 / 7.7e-5 of their domains' similarity mass (the bound is attained only when the tie holds ALL mass; the checker asserts Q <= bound fail-closed). The k=1 all-mass loci attain the k=1 bound exactly (both infinite). (2) Monotonicity: QUAL is a strictly monotone function of the share by construction (unit-proven) and measured monotone across all 35 loci; spot-check: chrMT tied locus7 share 1.37e-4 -> Q 0.00060 vs separated unique-top locus5 share 4.02e-3 -> Q 0.01751. (3) **The honest caveat: "unique separations give high Q" is FALSE in these measured domains** — a unique, well-separated top still holds only ~1e-4 of the domain's similarity mass because thousands of eligible near-twin classes each carry substantial cosine, so every realistic QUAL is near zero; high QUAL under this forced normalization requires the called class to dominate the domain's TOTAL similarity, which coalesced near-twin-rich domains never allow (only the 4 degenerate single-class domains are unbounded). QUAL does not track the best-vs-truth or best-vs-second delta across loci; it tracks the share. The owner must weigh this before the 502-locus stage — the formula is implemented exactly as ruled, with no free parameters, and the measured scale is what the forced normalization produces.

**Tie anatomy — bit-identical evidence, the numbers ((e) as redefined by the supervisor):** ALL 29 entries in the 1e-12 truth windows (chrMT 9, chrI 20) are BIT-EXACT (0 ULP). The non-truth tie partners are DISTINCT material classes (identical-signature duplicates were already removed by row coalescing): chrMT locus7's partner differs from the truth class's total usage by 6 node keys / 8 edge keys (multiplicity-preserving; equal counts); chrI locus2's two partners by 2/4 and 6/8; chrI locus14's partner by 2/4 — and EVERY differing segment/adjacency carries ZERO observed read mass (measured differing-observed-mass = 0.0, node and edge, in both the ties stream and the called-set emission). The bit-exactness is arithmetic identity, not coincidence: zero-mass differences contribute exact 0.0 dot addends and equal integer norm/overlap sums, so no read in this sample can break these ties — genuine ambiguity, PRESERVED (not created) by the condensation. The called-set k=2 ties (chrMT locus7: classes [113,150]/[113,151]; chrI locus14: [135,184]/[136,184], each pair sharing one row) have the identical structure: 6/8 and 2/4 usage-key differences, all zero observed mass. Member-level coalescing (the owner's "effectively the same" made countable): the 35 called sets carry 1–48 physical candidate pairs each; 150 chrMT + 197 chrI physical pairs collapse into 35 class calls (e.g., chrMT locus2's single called class = 12 physical pairs; chrI locus6's = 24).

## 2026-10-01 SESSION OPEN — DELTA-FORM QUAL (share form measured falsified; owner ruling: the similarity DELTA)
The share-form QUAL (committed 1f12d71) is measured FALSIFIED as a discrimination signal: it tracked the called set's share of total domain similarity mass (Q 0.0002–0.37 everywhere), not the similarity delta between the called set and its best alternative, giving no separation between a confident unique max and a genuine tie. Owner ruling: keep ALL stage-2 machinery EXACTLY as committed (material-class coalescing, called set = distinct classes at the bit-identical max, null emission for unbounded/massless loci, receipt checker, tie anatomy) and change ONLY the quality formula to p = s_win / (k*s_win + a), k = distinct material classes bit-tied at the max s_win, a = best similarity among ALL classes outside the called set (a = 0 if none); QUAL = -10*log10(1 - p). Derivation (forced two-way normalization k*s_win vs a, uniform truth within a bit-tied called set per the measured zero-differering-mass tie anatomy): confidence in the emitted single-material draw = (k*s_win/(k*s_win+a)) * (1/k) = s_win/(k*s_win+a). NO free parameters. Reduction checks i–iv, monotonicity in a and k, unit-proven k=2,3,5,9 all-mass ties adapted, receipts carry k/s_win/a per locus. Re-run exhaustive chrMT and chrI only; NO 502-locus stage. Implementation begins; increments follow.

**Increment 1 — formula swap implemented and unit-validated (commit f641c53).** `cosine_probe.rs` now computes the delta-form confidence `qual_p(s_win, k, a) = s_win/(k*s_win + a)` with `a` tracked as the largest strictly-below-maximum class similarity during the SAME exhaustive walk (a displaced maximum becomes the alternative), and emits per locus `qual_alternative_similarity` (null iff every class outside the called set carries no similarity value), `qual_delta_similarity` (s_win - a), `qual_p`, replacing `qual_share`; `qual_similarity_total` remains a diagnostic-only emission (the falsified form's denominator, NOT in the product formula). Stage-2 machinery untouched: coalescing, bit-identical called set, class signatures, ties stream, null emission. Checker phase I now explicitly reconciles the FALSIFIED share-form receipts (kept untouched on disk as the record) and new phase II reconciles the delta receipts against stage-1 AND against the share-form receipts' stage-2 fields (exact integers/booleans, documented ULP tolerance on HashMap-ordered floats), re-derives p by exact IEEE division, asserts unbounded-only-at-k=1-a=0, massless null, the k-way bound, cross-run ties-stream identity, measured monotonicity in a (across k=1 loci) and in k (counterfactual k=1 at the same s_win,a), and prints the per-locus delta tables plus the side-by-side distribution line. Design doc carries the full derivation (forced two-way normalization + uniform truth in the zero-differing-mass tied set) and the four reduction checks. Unit-measured: `cargo test --release --example panel_route_mem_routed cosine_` **13/13** (adapted all-mass k-way bound k=2,3,5,9 bit-identical at -10*log10(1-1/k); monotone in a and k; near-tie Q 3.0103; separated max Q>19.9; fail-closed unbounded/massless); build passed; checker py-compiles; runner `bash -n` and diff checks clean. chrMT delta pilot launching serially (receipts `run-cosine-graph-qual-delta-pilot-*`, output `cosine-graph-qual-delta-*.jsonl`; the falsified `cosine-graph-qual-*` receipts untouched).

**Reduction checks, measured (d):** (i) NO finite locus in these exhaustive domains has a clear gap — the best alternative sits at 90.7–99.99% of the maximum everywhere (widest: chrI locus15, a/s_win = 0.907, Q 3.23; narrowest: chrI locus2, delta 4.4e-5, Q 3.01); the only a = 0 loci are the four unbounded degenerate domains, so high Q occurs nowhere — an honest property of these domains, not a formula artifact. (ii) No measured k≥2 all-mass tie exists; both measured k=2 ties (chrMT 7, chrI 14) have a > 0 and land at Q 1.76, strictly below the all-mass k=2 bound 3.0103, checker-asserted; the bound itself is unit-proven bit-identically for k = 2, 3, 5, 9 (`qual_all_mass_k_way_class_tie_lands_at_derived_bound`, adapted: p = s_win/(k*s_win) = 1/k exactly for the chosen values). (iii) ALL 23 finite k=1 loci ARE near-twins (a ≈ s_win): Q 3.01–3.23, i.e. the -10*log10(1-1/2) = 3.0103 honest low is essentially attained everywhere — measured reality, not a synthetic case. (iv) Member coalescing never lowers Q: 150 (chrMT) + 197 (chrI) physical candidate pairs collapse into 33 single-class + 2 two-class calls (e.g. chrMT locus2's called class = 12 physical pairs, chrI locus6's = 24) with k unchanged, because the formula's only inputs are (s_win, k, a) — there is no member sum anywhere. **Monotonicity, measured:** across all k=1 loci p falls strictly as a/s_win rises (checker-asserted over the full sorted ratio sequence); at both k=2 loci the counterfactual k=1 confidence at the same (s_win, a) is strictly higher (chrMT 7: 0.5012 vs 0.3339; chrI 14: 0.5000 vs 0.3333).

**Quality distribution and the one-line comparison against the falsified share form (e):** falsified share form — 31 finite loci, Q 0.0001–0.370, median ~0.001, 4 unbounded, k=2 ties indistinguishable from unique maxima (0.0006 vs 0.0060); delta form — 31 finite loci, Q 1.76–3.23, median ~3.015, same 4 unbounded, k=2 ties at 1.76 clearly below the k=1 near-twin band 3.01–3.23. The delta form finally DISCRIMINATES (tie vs unique max, gap width via p) and reports the true state of these domains: every non-degenerate locus is a near-twin, so no call here deserves more than ~Q3 — consistent with the stage-1 finding of near-twin competitor classes above truth everywhere. Truth-in-called-set is UNCHANGED (2/8 chrMT: loci 2, 5; 1/17 chrI: locus 6): the formula change moves QUALITY, not the CALL.

## 2026-10-01 SESSION OPEN — CLUSTER-FORM QUAL (owner ruling: the near-twin runner-ups are not real alternatives; quality must measure separation from the NEXT ACTUALLY DIVERGENT CLUSTER, not the ninth twin). Plan: (1) material distance between candidate classes = observed mass on the symmetric difference of node+edge usage signatures, plus signature-cosine distance as a second view; (2) cluster cut = knee of the sorted winner-distance spectrum (max relative jump between consecutive distances; spectrum shape reported per locus, no-knee reported honestly); (3) QUAL = -10*log10(1 - p), p = s_win/(k*s_win + a) BETWEEN CLUSTERS (near-identical band members never supply a, never count in k); (4) reduction checks measured; (5) per-locus scientific crux: near-identical material (benign, Q re-rates high) vs divergent materials scoring equally (Q stays low) for every chrMT/chrI locus. Scope: cosine_probe.rs + check-cosine-graph-receipt.py, exhaustive chrMT/chrI re-run, NO 502-locus stage. Implementation begins; increments follow.

**Increment 1 — cluster-form QUAL implemented and unit-validated (commit 5c47b54).** `cosine_probe.rs` now computes, per locus on the combined arm after the unchanged exhaustive walk: (1) MATERIAL DISTANCE from the winner to every other eligible class = observed routed-share mass on the symmetric difference of the node+edge usage signatures (the `differing_observed_mass` helper, the same evidence units the cosines use), plus the SIGNATURE-COSINE distance (1 - cosine of the raw usage multisets, depth cancels) as a second view — both emitted as full sorted per-locus spectra (`qual_distance_spectrum` + aligned scores + signature distances) so where the cut lands is auditable evidence; (2) the CLUSTER CUT = the derived KNEE: the maximal relative jump (d_{i+1}-d_i)/d_{i+1} between consecutive STRICTLY-POSITIVE sorted distances (0/0:=0; ties to the largest index so the band absorbs the full dense run; cut = the last band point), with NO KNEE declared when the maximal jump does not exceed the MEDIAN jump (scale-free/geometric spectra) or fewer than two positive distances exist — then the cut stays at 0 (bit-identical innermost) and the nearest strictly-positive distance is emitted as the fallback measurement (`qual_nearest_rival_distance`); the exactly-zero band never pins the cut (a jump out of exact 0 is r=1 by definition and would split the band); (3) CLUSTER-LEVEL QUAL: k = single-linkage DIVERGENT components among the bit-tied called classes at the knee cut (chains included), a = best similarity OUTSIDE the union of the called classes' near-identity bands (each band emitted as `qual_tied_class_bands` for independent re-derivation), p = s_win/(k*s_win + a) via the unchanged `qual_p`, QUAL = -10*log10(1-p); members of the called cluster never supply a, never count in k; the best divergent rivals are NAMED per locus (`qual_alternative_classes` with identities, distances, both views); the second view's own knee summary is emitted as `qual_signature_view_*`. Called-set machinery, ties/competitors streams, walk and scoring UNMODIFIED (per-class called-set fields byte-identical in shape to the delta receipts). New unit tests: `spectrum_knee_derived_rule` (band+tail, zero-band never pins, geometric no-knee, tie-to-largest-index, flat-zero), `signature_cosine_distance_second_view` (hand-computed), `cluster_form_qual_owner_reductions` (all four owner reductions + single-linkage chains + cluster-level monotonicity in a + divergent bit-tie with tail); `cargo test --release --example panel_route_mem_routed cosine_` **16/16**; build passed. Checker phase III added to `check-cosine-graph-receipt.py`: re-derives the knee IN PYTHON from the emitted spectrum, cluster size, exclusion union, k (single-linkage), a, p (exact IEEE), QUAL, unbounded-only-at-k=1-a=0, k-way bound, alternative-class naming, zero-distance-never-supplies-a audit, second-view knee, absorbing-rival monotonicity, stage-1/2 reconciliation vs the delta receipts — plus the per-locus cluster tables and the delta-vs-cluster distribution line. `test-score-cosine-probe.py` 3/3, `bash -n`, `git diff --check` clean. chrMT pilot launched serially (receipts `run-cosine-graph-qual-cluster-pilot-*`, output `cosine-graph-qual-cluster-*.jsonl`; the delta receipts untouched on disk).
