The owner's named decision points from the pilot: (1) the floor's magnitude (ranks robust, gaps floor-dominated — the true unanchored floor ~−950/read would shrink gap numbers ~40% without changing orders); (2) the QUAL p-form's dynamic range under likelihood ratios (saturates unbounded — a form question, the machinery untouched per the ruling); (3) the row-extent seam effect (rows extending past a neighbor's end collect the straddlers — a row-geometry lever); (4) the within-anchor variant positions (the abstained remainder — measured 3.1bp/placement at the pilot, negligible).

## Session open (slice C launch: the generated-here/generated-elsewhere marginalization) - branch work/genome-mem-bwt-pipeline at dd15f37, tree clean of tracked changes. Scope per the owner's go: (1) MEASURE THE POCKET-READ ANATOMY AT L7 FIRST (partial vs continuous placement - it decides the form); (2) E(read) derived and documented (candidate-independent elsewhere branch, max-entropy background rate from the measured sample statistics, no tuning constants; floor becomes E(read), not 150*B); (3) the logsumexp mixture per (record, candidate) (+ the placement-continuity rule if the anatomy says partial); (4) re-run the three pilot loci (L4 control must hold; L2; L7 - prediction: the pocket harvest cancels or shrinks); (5) exactness/checker extensions; (6) walls, RSS, guard; commit per gate. THE EXHAUSTIVE chrMT/chrI RERUN IS NEXT SLICE - not started here. Implementation begins; increments follow.

**Increment 1 (slice C session) — DESIGN SETTLED against the artifacts; the anatomy emission decided as a rider on the scoring example (no new binary).** The committed slice-B receipt names L7's winner folds (ATV#3/#4 block43_contig1, BAD#3/#4 block96_contig1) and the gap decomposition (849 floor-mass + 54,015 per-base logs). THE PLAN: (1) an `--anatomy-out` mode on partition_realign_score.rs emitting, per locus, for every unit classified by (winner-places, truth-places): the per-placement anatomy on every pinning fold (collinearity = the pinned anchors' offset spread; m, c, backbone_bp, skipped_bp; the per-skipped-base votes for a sample), the unit's census occurrences (all panel paths), and the TRUTH-PATH elsewhere-origin verification (fetch the truth paths' sequences at the unit's truth-path occurrences and compare the FULL read, both strands - the elsewhere-copy full-match verdict, the generated-elsewhere evidence); (2) the verdict PARTIAL vs CONTINUOUS from the numbers; (3) then the marginalization: LL(read|fold) = logsumexp(local branch, E(read)), E = 150*A - ln(2*(G-149)) with G the MEASURED haploid genome size (the panel's own mean per-strain total path length - the donor's generation universe, max-entropy uniform origin over the genome's 2*(G-149) both-strand full-read placements at the measured per-base rate A - the same max-entropy spirit as beta = M/|U|: the unit of match likelihood spread uniformly over the placement universe; no tuning constants); the continuity (collinearity) rule if the anatomy says partial (non-collinear placements bounded at E - the piecewise serving's incompatible-offset credit was slice B's stated shifted-mismatch convenience; a substitution-only generation is one continuous path). KEY derived property, to be unit-proven: TRUE placements are always collinear (a read that is a contiguous segment of the row's source genome pins every anchor at its true offset - the true assignment is among the monotone assignments, so every pinned anchor sits at its true position), so the continuity rule never discounts a true placement; and the mixture's own bound absorbs every c>=1 placement (mA+cB <= -ln(2(L-149))-7.6 < E), so the E-floor plus continuity makes ONLY full-match continuous placements discriminate beyond E. Implementation begins.

## 2026-11-06 SLICE C COMPLETE — THE POCKET-READ ANATOMY (CONTINUOUS, WITH THE FRAME-BLINDNESS DISCOVERY), THE GENERATED-HERE/GENERATED-ELSEWHERE MARGINALIZATION, THE PILOT BEFORE/AFTER, THE CHECKER EXTENSION (COMMITTED 461e001 + bff1867)

**GATE 1 — THE ANATOMY (the form-deciding measurement, run FIRST; `--anatomy-out` on partition_realign_score, pure emission, exit 0, wall 45s, RSS peak 2.45GB; slice-B numbers reproduced bit-exact: rank 1,644, gap 1,247,492.61).** THE VERDICT: **CONTINUOUS** — the winner's pocket placements at L7 are **100% collinear** (one continuous path through the pocket row's spell), m mean **148.7/150** (c=0 on 75.5% of the 1,603 winner-only placements, c=1 on 17%, c>=10 only 24): the skipped/flank bases vote AGAINST THE SCAFFOLD ROWS' OWN CONTEXT and MATCH it — the pocket copies extend identical through the reads' full spans; NO read spans pocket-into-unique-flank material within its 150bp. So no placement-continuity rule is imposed; the marginalization carries the whole weight. Per the task's (a): the anchored fraction on the pocket is backbone mean 67.5bp + skipped mean 81.9bp + abstained 0.6bp = 150.

**THE ANATOMY'S HEADLINE DISCOVERY (escalated to the owner mid-slice, non-blocking): the pocket reads are NOT remote/foreign-generated.** EVERY one of the 1,603 winner-only-placed units (mass 1,677) has a FULL-READ 150/150 match on the sample's own donor paths S288C#0#chrI / SK1#0#chrI (the anatomy's donor-check, origin-shift-corrected, strand-aware, per unit): **1,051 units (66%) match INSIDE window 7's own truth rows** (the truth folds' rows spell them exactly), 426 (27%) in the neighboring ±2kb seam, only 126 far. Yet the truth folds place NONE of the in-window ones: **2,059 units (mass 2,140) at L7 have an in-window full-match donor occurrence with NO valid truth placement.** THE CAUSE, proven with a named example (record 113 / unit 84 / node 92071, cross-checked against the fold's committed walk): the fold PIN SKELETONS are built from `walk_path_range` — ONE frame's syncmer selection — and reads whose anchor k-mers are **rc-frame-qualified on the truth path are ABSENT from that walk** (slice A's binding lesson, recorded as a warning for slice B, missed in the pin skeleton; the stored-frame walk carries OTHER syncmer windows at those positions — nodes 7446960/9705993/7362615 at the anchor's window) while the same k-mers appear canonical-forward on the reverse-complement scaffold copies, which pin and collect the full match at orientation 1. **The truth's 150*B floor for these reads is a PLACEMENT-MACHINERY FRAME ARTIFACT, not an inability to spell them.** The slice-B "54,015 per-base logs" also decompose into one-homolog floor asymmetries (±1,546-magnitude mixture terms — 973 mass winner-places/truth-floors vs 828 mass the reverse), not per-base evidence: on jointly-placed reads the truth's per-base evidence is BETTER (m 146.8 / c 1.08 vs the winner's 142.9 / 2.93). Pocket-unit anchor counts: 69% single-anchor reads (the most frame-vulnerable class).

**GATE 2-3 — THE MARGINALIZATION (the owner's design, verbatim; implemented in examples/partition_realign_score.rs, assessment-side, no src/ file touched, no thresholds, no tuning constants):** LL(read | candidate) = logsumexp( the local-spell branch (the existing per-(record,row) likelihood: the uniform placement prior + the anchored placements' backbone + skipped-base votes) , E(read) ). **THE E-DERIVATION, documented** (the example header, the docs note, the receipt's scoring block, checker-audited): E(read) = len(read)·A − ln(2·(G_diploid − len(read) + 1)) — the read explained by the genome outside this locale at the MEASURED per-base rate (the census's zero-unmatched-bp measurement over 1,063,522 verified occurrences: reads are exact segments of their origins, at the reads' own Phred-40-derived rate A) under the max-entropy uniform origin over the donor's diploid genome — **the same max-entropy spirit as the old Poisson model's beta = M/|U|**: the unit of match likelihood spread uniformly over the universe of placements; G measured TRUTH-FREE from the panel (the instrument's own genome universe): the mean per-(strain,haplotype) total path length **14,199,901.5 bp over 235 haplotype path sets, doubled for the diploid = 28,399,803 bp; E = −17.870035**. THE FLOOR for unexplained reads is E, not 150*B (which survives only as the per-placement lower bound inside the local branch's logsumexp — a bit-exact no-op where anchors pin). A read whose local placements score no better than E does not vote beyond E; **logsumexp2 is monotone in the local branch — the L4-control preservation property, unit-proven** (a candidate at least as good as every rival per unit keeps its ordering; identical folds stay identical). Unit tests 14/14 (the anatomy collinearity both strands + the true-placement-always-collinear property, the one-continuous-path score == the piecewise score on collinear placements, the E derivation, the floor bounding, the monotonicity/tie preservation, all slice-B tests).

**THE PILOT BEFORE/AFTER (chrI 2,4,7; exit 0, wall 65s, RSS peak 2.65GB, 64GiB guard clean; 768,827/768,827 factorized placements exact):**

| locus | truth rank before → after | log gap before → after | winner before → after |
|---:|---|---:|---|
| **4 (the control)** | **1 → 1** | **0.0 → 0.0** | unchanged (bit-exact, checker-enforced) |
| 2 | 4 → 2 | 151,917.18 → 60.74 | unchanged (the BTE identical fold + S288C/W303 fold) |
| 7 | 1,644 → **8** | 1,247,492.61 → **982.04** | (ATV+BAD scaffold pockets) → (the S288C/AAA/SGDref fold + the ATV pocket fold) |

The pocket harvest shrinks **99.92%** at L7; the winner stops being the pure scaffold-pocket pair; the both-placed evidence gap flips negative (−2,900.7: the truth's evidence is better on jointly-placed reads); the identical-through-graph fold stays ONE candidate by construction (checker-verified from the GFAs — the non-vote stays a non-vote); the truth's floor penalty is bounded by E everywhere (the log_gap_decomposition attribution updated); QUAL stays unbounded at all three loci (the p-form's relative likelihoods underflow at the surviving separations; machinery unmodified per the ruling).

|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|

| E derivation | 0.002s | ~0% | 0.002s | ~0% | 235 haplotype sets |
| derive cache | 1.4s | 0.9% | 1.4s | 0.2% | 2,439,103 reads / 666,327 keys |
| **BINDING (anchor/pin placement + verify)** | **134.5s** | **91.1%** | **551.1s** | **93.6%** | 4,891/13,905 records; 234,472/1,415,203 occurrences; 4,954/14,629 candidate keys; **703,605/4,247,781 verify probes; 708,496/4,261,686 AGC fetches (51.4/308.5 MB)** |
| read variants | 0.0s | ~0% | 0.4s | 0.1% | per-record variant census |
| rows (context assembly) | 1.5s | 1.0% | 6.3s | 1.1% | 1,252/4,472 rows; 2,504/8,944 fetches (16.5/78.2 MB) |
| loci: fold enum + units/locality + skeleton sidecar | 0.6s | 0.4% | 2.0s | 0.3% | Σ 1,099/3,545 folds; Σ 47,997/266,095 units |
| loci: scoring matrix | 0.2s | 0.1% | 1.9s | 0.3% | 1,929,497/21,490,054 placements (rayon) |
| loci: class LLs | 0.1s | 0.1% | 3.0s | 0.5% | Σ 59,225/378,306 classes × units (~4.9e9 mix_logsumexp terms at chrI) |
| loci: QUAL + exactness | 0.3s | 0.2% | 1.4s | 0.2% | spectra + 3,360/5,178 sample pairs |
| loci: receipts/IO | 0.9s | 0.6% | 12.1s | 2.1% | the ingredients ll_matrix (chrI L16: 34.5M entries) |

**THE INTERPRETATION (the dominance is absolute and one-sided):** within binding, the verify probes are **99.1% (chrMT, 133.3s) / 99.5% (chrI, 547.9s)** of the phase — i.e. **~90%/93% of the entire instrument wall is the per-(occurrence x shift x candidate) sequence-fetch verification loop of the record-binding phase**; the step extraction (0.8/2.3s) and key-shape derivations (0.0/0.1s) are noise. The unit cost is CALL-bound, not byte-bound: the average fetched verify range is ~72 bp (308.5 MB over 4,261,686 fetches at chrI) at ~129-190 µs/probe — the per-call random-access overhead of `sources.fetch` (per-call seek/decompress into the route sources). **THE LEVER MAPPING, measured:** (a) **hoist-to-once candidate-independent recomputation — THE lever:** the verify loop re-fetches the same per-occurrence range once per shift (and per candidate): **4,247,781 probes vs 1,415,203 occurrence-ranges = a measured 3.01x pure re-fetch redundancy** (candidate multiplicity is small — 14,629 candidates / 13,905 records — so the 3-shift loop IS the redundancy); hoisting the fetch to once per (occurrence, candidate) bounds the dominant phase at ~1/3 of its wall (~183s at chrI) with NO model change, and a range-fetch cache (the binding ranges overlap the row sequences the rows phase fetches anyway) collapses the per-call overhead further; second-order members measured: the rows phase's 2-fetches-per-row (8,944 for 4,472 rows, 6.3s), the per-(fold,unit) decode_record_tokens re-derivation (inside the 1.9s scoring wall). (b) **per-class re-derivation (the Phase-2 vote-vector lever): real CPU but 0.1%/0.5% of wall** (~4.9e9 mix_logsumexp terms at chrI, rayon-parallel — the wall is the measure). (c) **irreducible:** the semantic 1,415,203 occurrence verifications + 21,490,054 placements — even fully hoisted, binding bounds at ~1/3; the remaining cost is the fetch mechanism itself. **THE MEMORY-PEAK ATTRIBUTION (the 5.66 GiB, now measured directly):** NOT binding (chrI RSS 3.21 GB) and NOT scoring (4.27 GB at L16) — the peak is held in the **per-locus receipts/IO phase at the large-matrix loci**: the ingredients sidecar materializes the full ll_matrix as a serde_json value tree before writing (chrI L16: 448 folds x 77,117 units = 34.5M entries ≈ +1.15 GB live over the 4.27 GB baseline; the clean rerun's poller caught 5,957,684 kB there; the post-write probe reads 4.80 GB retained). A streaming serializer (to_writer over the matrix without the value-tree materialization) would remove the spike without touching receipt content — named as the memory lever, NOT implemented here.

**The checker in full (all modes, ALL PHASES PASS, 0 failures everywhere):** the new `--exhaustive chrMT|chrI --timered-base <base> --run-tag <tag>` mode re-runs every slice-E phase over the timered receipts and adds **phase 8t THE ANSWER-PRESERVATION GATE** (per locus every semantic field equal to the committed slice-E receipt, only walls/rss_kb excluded, the walls field set checked unchanged; the four sidecars byte-identical): chrMT 1,170,642 checks, chrI 4,342,914 checks, 0 failures — the +730/+896 checks over slice E are phase 8t's own. The three prior modes unchanged on their committed receipts: default 6,760, --marginal 6,592, --frame 579,972, all 0 failures (the checker extension is a no-op there). Checker logs check-realign-timered-{chrMT,chrI}.log + check-realign-priormode*.log at the validation dir. Receipts: realign-timered-{chrMT,chrI}.{jsonl,exactness.jsonl,skeleton.jsonl} + sidecars + run markers (run-realigntimered-*); the committed slice-E receipts untouched on disk as the record.

## Session open (phase 3 of the runtime plan: THE PARALLELISM PILOT, MEASURED NOT ASSUMED) - branch work/genome-mem-bwt-pipeline at 4392c6d, tree clean of tracked changes. History: the serial-only rule comes from a MEASURED race in the OLD machinery's reference-rescore (7-wide and 2-wide both failed, solo passed; the race file parked, one line). This instrument is receipt-side and partition-local, but the discipline is measure-don't-assume. Scope: (1) identify the natural parallel seams in the phase-1 profile (the binding lane loads + verify loop, the loci loop, the per-record scoring - the phases that now dominate: loci loop 11.4s + binding 10.0s at chrI) and PROVE the shared state each seam touches is not mutable; (2) THE 2-WIDE PILOT over exhaustive chrI + chrMT, race detector = byte-identity of receipts + all four sidecars + every semantic field vs the phase-1 serial receipts (the identity-gate pattern); (3) IF CLEAN widen 4/8-wide, same gate each rung, measuring box load first; IF NOT CLEAN stop at 2-wide, name the shared state, park it; (4) THE WALL TABLE serial vs 2 vs 4 vs 8 per phase, both components, external RSS poller each rung, 64GiB guard; (5) checker all modes over the parallel receipts; unit tests; (6) the honest scaling note (chrIV-class chromosome + whole genome, labeled extrapolation). Commit per rung. Implementation begins; increments follow.

**Increment 1 (phase-3 session) — THE SEAMS INSTRUMENTED + THE SERIAL RE-BASELINE PASSES THE IDENTITY GATE (chrMT, exit 0, wall 18s, poller peak 2,314,168kB, 64GiB guard clean).** The two natural parallel seams in the phase-1 profile, in examples/partition_realign_score.rs only: SEAM 1 = the per-record binding loop (10.0s of 35.4s at chrI; lane loads + verify probes); SEAM 2 = the per-locus loop (11.4s at chrI; fold enum, locality, scoring, classes, QUAL, receipts/IO). THE SHARED-STATE PROOF (compiler-enforced, not asserted): each seam body is a pure per-record / per-locus function — every outer capture is an immutable shared reference (the panel, the census lines, the key indexes, the derive cache, the reads, the partition maps, the assembled row store, the bound keys/shifts/variants) or the thread-safe fetch path (the AtomicU64 fetch counters; the LaneCache's Mutex-per-lane slots — the lane load holds its lane's mutex through Sources::fetch, so loads are serialized per lane and idempotent, and no lane can load twice) — the &dyn Fn in RowStore is widened to &(dyn Fn + Sync), which the rayon par_iter bound then ENFORCES at compile time; every seam output lands in its own per-record / per-locus slot and is merged serially in iteration order. THE EMISSION DISCIPLINE: every sidecar line (skeleton per fold, exactness per sampled pair, anatomy per unit, records, the streamed ingredients, the main receipt) is buffered per locus and written in LOCUS ORDER after the loop, so parallel execution cannot interleave lines — the receipts are interleaving-independent BY CONSTRUCTION. THE WIDTH CONTROL: IMPG_REALIGN_PARALLEL_SEAMS=1 switches the two seams to the rayon pool; RAYON_NUM_THREADS=N is the rung; the committed default (no env) is the serial path, byte-for-byte. Unit tests 17/17. THE SERIAL RE-BASELINE (the seam code, no env vars, chrMT all 14 loci): the four sidecars BYTE-IDENTICAL to the committed phase-1 rebuilt receipts (exactness/ingredients/records/skeleton), every semantic field equal (0 diffs; only walls/rss_kb differ), all binding counters identical (4,891 records / 234,472 occurrences / 4954 candidates / 703,605 verify probes over 234,535 range fetches / 141 lane loads) — the refactor is a proven no-op on the serial path. Runner: genome/instrumented/run-realign-parallel.sh (the house pattern + the width env). The 2-wide pilot next.

**Increment 2 (phase-3 session) — THE FIRST 2-WIDE ATTEMPT MEASURED A PATHOLOGY; THE SEAM MECHANISM CORRECTED (dedicated seam threads); THE 2-WIDE PILOT IS CLEAN ON BOTH COMPONENTS.** THE MEASURED FINDING (the discipline held — measure, don't assume): the first 2-wide run put the seams on the rayon pool with RAYON_NUM_THREADS=2, and the chrMT loci phase went 1.8s -> 18.5s. The control experiment isolated TWO stacked effects: (1) the committed "serial" runs were never single-threaded in the loci loop — the per-locus scoring/classes/spectrum par_iters ride rayon's DEFAULT pool (the whole box), and bounding the pool to the rung width silently serialized THOSE phases too (serial code at RAYON_NUM_THREADS=2: loci 8.2s, still 4.5x over the 1.8s default-pool wall); (2) nesting the seam par_iter in the SAME bounded pool adds contention on top (18.5s: two concurrent loci each enqueueing thousands of inner jobs onto the same 2 workers). NO RACE: even that pathological run's receipts were byte-identical, all four sidecars, 0 semantic diffs. THE CORRECTED MECHANISM (seam_map, in-code documented): the seams run on DEDICATED scoped std::threads at width N (stride-assigned, per-item result slots read out in item order — interleaving-independent by construction), and from a non-rayon thread every inner par_iter installs rayon's GLOBAL pool — the committed inner behavior, unchanged. THE 2-WIDE PILOT (the corrected mechanism, both components, exit 0, 64GiB guard clean): chrMT wall 14s (serial re-baseline 18s) poller peak 2,329,120kB; chrI wall 34s (serial re-baseline 41s, committed phase-1 37s) poller peak 5,519,472kB — THE IDENTITY GATE PASSES BOTH: the receipts equal the committed phase-1 rebuilt receipts on every semantic field (0 diffs, only walls/rss_kb), all FOUR sidecars BYTE-IDENTICAL (exactness/ingredients/records/skeleton), binding counters identical (chrI 13,905 records / 1,415,203 occurrences / 14,629 candidates / 4,247,781 verify probes over 1,415,927 range fetches / 840 lane loads). THE PHASE PICTURE at chrI: binding 9.7s serial -> 6.8s at 2-wide (the 840 lane loads serialize behind their per-lane mutexes — the load phase is the floor); loci 13.1s serial -> 14.7s at 2-wide (a small LOSS — two live loci contend on the shared global pool and memory; measured, not assumed). chrMT serial re-baseline receipts also byte-identical (rung 0, committed ea64cca). Widening to 4 next, box load checked each rung.

(2) THE REPAIR (exact, no thresholds): anchor_correspondence's pinned vector is the SAME closed form computed by a forward/backward FEASIBILITY DP over the candidate-bearing anchor chain - every complete monotone chain contains EVERY candidate-bearing anchor (the recursion dies otherwise), so a position is pinned iff it is the anchor's UNIQUE distinct feasible candidate (forward-feasible AND backward-feasible); two feasible candidates = the leaves disagree = None (ambiguous); no feasible candidate anywhere = zero leaves = all-None (the DP reproduces it because no candidate is then both-way feasible); anchors with no candidates stay None. Cost O(anchors x candidates) instead of exponential. (3) THE EQUIVALENCE GATES, all passed: the 18th unit test (3,000 randomized synthetic walks x both orientations - repeat nodes, empty candidates, zero-chain cases, duplicate positions) compares the DP against a VERBATIM COPY of the committed enumeration and caught one real divergence on the way (duplicate candidate entries at one bp - the enumeration pins the agreed position, the DP first counted entries; fixed by counting DISTINCT positions; real walks cannot produce duplicates but exactness is exactness) - now 18/18; and the REAL-DATA BYTE-IDENTITY GATES: with the DP build, realign chrMT + chrI serial (14+21 loci) reproduce the committed phase-1 rebuilt receipts with ALL FOUR SIDECARS BYTE-IDENTICAL and 0 semantic diffs in the main receipts (walls/rss_kb excluded by the gate's convention). ## Session open (THE CALLED-VS-TRUTH SEQUENCE QV - the owner's correction of the log-gap "QV-like" pattern) - worker W3 on branch work/genome-mem-bwt-pipeline, tree clean at 9f33730, 16 of 17 components closed (chrIX EXCLUDED - its exhaustive run still in flight). ## Session open (THE CAUSAL AUTOPSY OF THE 562 NON-RANK-1 CALLS - the owner's demand: WHY are they called with gap-dominated ~10% structural divergence) - branch work/genome-mem-bwt-pipeline at 714c20c, tree clean of tracked changes. chrIX EXCLUDED (in flight). THE HYPOTHESIS TO MEASURE (stated as hypothesis, not assumption): EXTENT ASYMMETRY - partition/window boundaries fragment the truth's in-domain rows while rival rows span longer across the seams, collecting orphaned read mass (the unplaced-mass advantage), so the likelihood correctly prefers the longer explainer and the QV comparison over mismatched extents produces end-gaps. THE AUTOPSY PER NON-RANK-1 LOCUS (receipt-side only): (1) the extent comparison (called fold's covered intervals vs truth fold's - lengths, overlap, overhang fractions); (2) the alignment gap anatomy from the biWFA CIGARs (end-gaps vs interior gaps vs mismatches, per-locus split in bases); (3) the likelihood decomposition from log_gap_decomposition (the unplaced-mass term vs the both-placed evidence term - who wins each); (4) the census class + row-geometry facts (partition-boundary cuts, seam splits, tiling gaps, contig-end - the cut points named); (5) THE AGGREGATE VERDICT (what fraction of the 562 each cause explains - extent-asymmetry-driven, repeat-domain substitution, contig-end, other; per-cause QV profile); (6) THE FIX MAPPING (each cause's named lever with the measured locus count each is expected to convert to rank-1, honest ordering). NO FIXES IMPLEMENTED - measurement only. Commit the autopsy script + receipts + the report section. THE MACHINERY (assessment-side, receipt-streamed, the ingredients sidecars never opened): `genome/instrumented/nonrank1-autopsy.py` + the biWFA helper's optional `--cigar` mode (the default protocol BYTE-IDENTICAL to the committed QV stage's — the QV stage never passes the flag; the flag appends the walked CIGAR op string as a fifth field). THE PER-LOCUS AUTOPSY, the six demanded measurements: (1) THE EXTENT COMPARISON — the called folds' and truth folds' covered rows and lengths (fold_identities), called_len_sum vs truth_len_sum, the per-end overhangs IN BASES from the alignment (leading/trailing gap columns per side), the aligned overlap; (2) THE ALIGNMENT GAP ANATOMY from the walked CIGARs — the leading/trailing gap-column runs are END-GAPS (the extent overhang; 'D' cols consume the called sequence, 'I' cols the truth), the interior gap columns are TRUE MATERIAL SUBSTITUTION, mismatches counted separately, the interior gap runs, the end-gap share of edits; (3) THE LIKELIHOOD DECOMPOSITION — the receipt's own `both_placed_evidence_gap` (winner-minus-truth over the units BOTH pairs place) and `residual_unplaced_gap` = log_gap − that term (EXACTLY the winner-minus-truth over the units not both place; units neither places score at the same derived E and cancel), the unplaced masses/units, the E-scale of the asymmetry (e_scale_mass_asym = mass_asym × E, the stated magnitude scale, NOT the exact LL), who wins each term; (4) THE CENSUS CLASS + row geometry — the census verdict re-derived from the census receipts, the window, the axis partition, the SK1 window ortholog's tiling placement partitions (THE CUT POINTS NAMED PER LOCUS: census_ortholog_partitions), the truth folds' member rows and SK1 membership; (5)+(6) the aggregate verdict and fix mapping below.

THE EXTENT MEASUREMENT: the called diplotype is LONGER than the truth at 513/562 (91.3%), median len_delta +1,834bp, median called end-overhang 1,378bp vs truth end-overhang 0bp; 68 loci carry overhangs on BOTH sides (the shifted-row shape — the rival row shifted across the seam, not merely longer); the median end-gap share of all edits 75.1% (the QV stage's 98%-gap-columns fact refined: of the gap columns, the END-GAPS dominate at the median).

THE LIKELIHOOD TERM WINNERS (the hypothesis's likelihood half, measured at cohort scale): the unplaced-differential term is WINNER-favored at 560/562 (99.6%) — the winner collects orphaned read mass the truth cannot place (median asymmetry 2,215 reads; E-scale median 39,582 LL) at essentially every non-rank-1 locus; the both-placed evidence term is TRUTH-favored at 494/562 (87.9%, median magnitude 2,893 LL — 14x smaller than the E-scale of the unplaced term); |residual| > |both_placed| at 551/562; the median residual share of log_gap 1.34. THE LIKELIHOOD IS CORRECT TO PREFER THE LONGER EXPLAINER GIVEN THE DOMAIN — the jointly placed reads already prefer the truth, and the orphaned mass the fragmented truth cannot place carries the call. This reproduces and generalizes slice E's chrI/chrMT residuals exactly (chrMT L6 +1,857 / L10 +1,312 / L9 +457 unplaced asymmetries against truth-favoring both-placed evidence at those loci; chrI L6/L12's longer-rival-row winners).

THE ANATOMY HALF: end-gap-dominated (end-gap cols > interior gap cols) at 314/562 (55.9%); interior-gap-dominated at 248 (44.0%, median 5 interior gap runs — true material substitution, the diverged-copy class); the FULL HYPOTHESIS SHAPE (unplaced-mass advantage AND truth-favored shared evidence AND end-gap-dominated anatomy) at 299/562 (53.2%, the contig-end locus included in the shape but assigned to its structural class by precedence).

THE CAUSAL DECOMPOSITION (primary cause per locus, the stated precedence: contig-end class first, then the extent shape S1+S2+end-dominated, then the interior shape S1+S2+interior-dominated, then rival-dominant S1+not-S2, then the no-S1 shared-evidence rival, then the boundary shapes): **extent-asymmetry 298 (53.0%, median QV 9.48, median identity 88.72%, median end-gap share 98.2%, median mass asym 932, median len_delta +2,358bp)** — the hypothesis's class, the QV is exactly the comparison over mismatched extents; **repeat-domain substitution 194 (34.5%, median QV 12.82, identity 94.78%, end-gap share 0.2%, mass asym 11,140, len_delta +680bp)** — the same unplaced-mass mechanism but the called fold is a diverged copy (interior substitution: the QV measures true sequence divergence, not extent mismatch); **rival-dominant 66 (11.7%, QV 12.32, both-placed gap +4,772 median — the rival wins the jointly placed reads too; by class: 30 seam / 27 in-axis / 6 foreign-repeat / 2 pilot / 1 partial)**; **shared-evidence rival 2 (chrIII L5 QV 40.03 and chrXVI L2 QV 39.89, both 99.99% identity — the owner's "effectively the same" calls: a near-identical rival winning on jointly placed reads alone, NO unplaced advantage)**; **boundary 1 (chrXIV L79: 146 edits, all mismatches, zero gaps)**; **contig-end 1 (QV 7.96, end-gap share 95.7%)**. The cause x census-class cross-tab: extent-asymmetry is dominated by in-axis loci (243 of 298) — the partition-boundary cut is the ambient geometry (the SK1 window ortholog tiles >1 partition at 258/287 census-covered extent loci, median 2 partitions), NOT confined to the seam class (33 seam / 11 foreign-repeat / 11 pilot); the seam class (tiled-elsewhere/neighbor, 101) splits 33 extent / 37 interior / 30 rival-dominant / 1 boundary.

THE FIX MAPPING (NO FIXES IMPLEMENTED; the conversion criterion stated: a mass-re-attribution repair converts a locus iff with the unplaced-differential term neutralized the both-placed evidence decides for the truth, i.e. both_placed_evidence_gap < 0 — a measured upper bound under FULL neutralization): **extent normalization of the comparison domain: 265 addressed, 265 expected conversions** (the extent-asymmetry class minus its seam members); **repeat-domain repair: 157 addressed, 157 conversions** (the interior class minus its seam members); **partition-spine seam repair: 101 addressed, 71 conversions** (the seam class: 71 with truth-favoring shared evidence; the 30 rival-dominant seam loci need more than seam geometry); **contig-end handling: 1 addressed, 1 conversion**; **no mass-re-attribution lever: 38 loci** (the rival-dominant non-seam remainder + the two shared-evidence rivals — the rival wins the jointly placed reads; a candidate-universe/evidence-model question, not a geometry repair). TOTAL CONVERTIBLE under full neutralization: 494/562 (87.9%). THE HONEST ORDERING: extent normalization (265) > repeat-domain repair (157) > seam repair (71) > contig-end handling (1) — with the caveat that the ordering is by expected conversions under the stated criterion, the per-lever sets overlap only through the seam class (the precedence assigns each locus one lever), and the residual term's exact one-side-placed LLs are not in the receipt (the criterion uses only the receipt-exact both-placed and residual terms).

Commit per gate. Implementation begins; increments follow.

**Increment 1 (the extent-normalization session) — THE RULE DERIVED FROM DIRECT MEASUREMENT, NOT CHOSEN: the naive window-territory witness FALSIFIED first (the census's own per-occurrence touched-window sets — partitions==[L] — measure ~ZERO out-of-territory mass at chrMT: 26-86 units of thousands per locus, and the chrMT gate prediction under that rule is CONVERT 0 / REGRESS 0, receipt-side exact via the ll_matrix); the geometry measured at the failing loci names the real defect: the census's window territory is the PANGENOME territory (every path's full BED row — the CBK/CBM row [10519,17115) IS window 2's territory row), so every candidate row sits wholly 'in-territory' while the candidates' OWN row extents differ by thousands of bp (chrMT L2: the winner's first homolog fold 73 = CBK/CBM rows len 6,596 vs the truth axis fold 109 len 2,231 — the winner shares its SECOND homolog (the SK1 fold) with the truth and swaps the first for a 3x-longer row; L6: DBVPG row 10,955 vs SK1 5,852; L10: W303 row 6,911 vs axis 2,587); THE TERRITORY-IMAGE RULE derived: the normalized comparison domain is the LOCUS'S OWN COORDINATE — the window's axis row; per fold its territory image = the interval spanned by the fold's walk steps SHARED with the axis row's walk (the alignment-induced correspondence, the instrument's own committed node-identity structure — the same semantics the pin machinery already trusts); placements and the uniform placement prior are restricted to the image; out-of-image mass falls to the CANDIDATE-INDEPENDENT E branch for BOTH candidates equally (it cannot vote for the spanner). MEASURED COHERENCE at the failing loci: the images are near-equal in length across frames (L2: 2,215/2,231/2,310 vs axis 2,231; L6: 5,858/5,616/5,852; L10: 2,471/2,508/2,471) while the winner rows' extra spans are thousands of bp OUTSIDE their images (L2 fold 73: image [4381,6596) — the leading 4,381bp carries NO window-2 node; L6 fold 110: image [5097,10955); L10 fold 110: image [4440,6911)) — the orphaned-mass harvest span is exactly the out-of-image extension. THE HONEST CHECK stated: the territory is the locus's own coordinate, not the truth's and not any candidate's; a true insertion/deletion haplotype's material WITHIN the window keeps full local evidence (the image is the interval BETWEEN the outermost shared anchors — interior diverged pockets are included); its material beyond the window is E for every candidate equally, so it loses no RELATIVE evidence and no rival can harvest it. The prediction machinery (territory-normalization-predict.py, receipt-side, the ll_matrix exactness argument) is committed to the falsification record. Implementation of the env-gated rule (IMPG_REALIGN_TERRITORY_NORMALIZED) next; the chrMT/chrI gate follows.

**Increment 2 (the extent-normalization session) — THE RULE IMPLEMENTED ENV-GATED (IMPG_REALIGN_TERRITORY_NORMALIZED, beside the committed convention) AND THE CHRMT/CHRI GATES PASSED.** (1) THE IMPLEMENTATION (partition_realign_score.rs, rule 8 of the model doc): per locus the territory images are derived from the folds' own walks (the interval spanned by the steps shared with the window's axis row's walk; empty image for a fold sharing no territory anchor); the placement-validity domain and the uniform prior's support become the image (under the default convention every fold's image IS its full extent and the code path degenerates EXACTLY to the committed comparison); out-of-image mass falls to the candidate-independent E branch for both candidates equally. THE HONEST VERDICT (the owner's question - is the pggb substrate the right build going forward, and does alignment induction help the remaining 193 seam/repeat-interior loci?): (a) THE SUBSTRATE IS PROVEN AND SAFE: coordinate-continuous with the global syng interning at 6,956/6,956 rows (spell equality, the id-continuity discipline), member-row-identical (56 partitions, 0 mismatches), answer-neutral for the committed row-sequence likelihood (receipts field-identical, all four sidecars md5-identical, both components, serial and 4-wide) - the instrument, anchor projection and truth index work unchanged over it, so adopting it costs nothing and risks nothing; # THE CLOSING SECTION — the whole arc's measured chain, from the windowed frame's broken domains to the final state

## The starting injury, measured (2026-09-29/30)
The windowed reference frame excluded the SK1 truth pair at **805 of 1,307 windows**; the product called ONE molecule everywhere (the haploid single-slot product failure, 0/3 and 0/10 two-slot calls at the smoke gates); only **4/502** expressible windows recovered the exact truth pair; the injective diploid genotype distance measured **0.5128** on the 6x/4x sample (v2 balanced 15x/15x: **0.3001**) - a sequence improvement, not correct diploid identities. The reference frame itself was the measured limitation (the dual-frame / proportional-coordinate problem, the partition-native spine named as the correction path).

## The realignment instrument (the per-locus marginal likelihood over the graph panel)
The per-locus realignment likelihood replaced the frame-dependent candidate domain: both homologs present and jointly spellable per window, the truth pair in the candidate universe. The committed default convention scored **549/1,133 expressible = 48.5%** truth rank-1 - the honest baseline the whole arc improved against.

## Fix 1 - the extent normalization (the territory-image rule, Stage 2)
The territory-image extent normalization (the over-long partition rows truncated to their real images) converted the extent class: **876/1,133 = 77.3%**, CONVERT 327 / REGRESS 0 (zero-regression guard holding fleet-wide), QV median 17.77 -> 60.00, perfect 874/1,133, QV>=40 77.41%. The wall-fix (the degenerate-locus tie certificate) and the checker-hygiene repair (the append-mode marker disease) landed inside this stage.

## Fix 2 - the alignment-induced locality domain (the owner's go: "try out the thing you think is gonna work")
The domain family that names its own universe from real alignment: the locality = the connected component of the axis row's PGBB graph; the fold = the path-interval hull of the maximal monotone axis-correspondence (pggb shared segments + interned syng nodes chained along the window spine, bounded to the component's own axis partitions); the over-join separation and the seam rejoins measured first (gate 1), the construction repaired on the gate's own evidence (the per-path fold merging broke the identical-copy coalescing - the chrMT L2/L7/L9/L10 regressions caught and repaired; the +K+W margin break), the no-op proof landed (the env-unset default path reproduces the committed receipts with 0 diffs). Gates 1-2 closed at chrMT/chrI: HOLD 9/9 + 14/14, ALL PHASES PASS 5,283,055/0 combined.

## Gate 3 - the fleet under the locality domains (17/17)
The substrate: 2,715 partition graphs built across two converging lanes (the full 1358-partition union todo, redundant-but-complete), **271,377/271,377 member rows spell-equal** to their export GFA P lines - the fleet's coordinates proven to be the syng coordinates. The chain per component: locality maps -> serial -> 4-wide -> the identity gate (0 semantic diffs, all four sidecars md5-identical, everywhere) -> the checker slices (--territory --locality, phase 8N's BEFORE pointed at the committed territory receipts). Six checker repair classes surfaced and were repaired live through the sweep, each unit-tested + mirror-gated byte-identical on the committed chrMT receipts (mirrors 1-6), the drivers marker-idempotent; the degenerate loci (chrVII L56, chrXII L86) passed live on the committed empty-product guards, and chrVII L83 (the one Poisson-expressible locus lost) honestly carried by the committed loss guard.

## THE FINAL STATE - the honest scoreboard, every number (17/17 components, 1,307 loci)
- **Truth rank-1: 880/1,132 expressible = 77.7%** (default convention 549/1,133 = 48.5% -> territory 876/1,133 = 77.3% -> locality 880/1,132 = 77.7%)
- **Expressibility: 1,133 -> 1,132** - gained 5 (chrX L50, chrXIV L9, chrXVI L8/L85/L93, the seam chain), lost 6 (chrV L59, chrVII L56+L83, chrXII L101, chrXV L49, chrXVI L7, the exact rule's honest cost), every move named
- **The conversion count vs the 193 addressable prediction: REFUTED - CONVERT 9 / REGRESS 5 / moved 350** (the 9 all unique wins by cause extent 4 / repeat-domain 4 / rival-dominant 1; the chrI-L13 interior-repeat mechanism measured genome-wide: the winners are connected in-component repeat-copy rows placing genuinely-better-matched high-multiplicity reads; rejoining severed ortholog material does not make the truth pair win - the advantage lives in the evidence model, not the geometry)
- **The 5 regressions** (the design's "any regression = design failure, named" contract, honored): the edge-pocket/hull-sensitivity class (chrIII L20 gap 0.27, chrXI L39 gap 0.23, chrXV L15 gap 0.21 - near-twin rival hulls at truncated truth folds; chrIII L35 gap 162.5 - the seam chain admitted BAQ_1a's 351bp-longer REAL ortholog) + the over-join separation class (chrVII L56, the degenerate 15bp contig-end window, the truth pair inexpressible)
- **QV re-run: n=1,132, PERFECT 880** (= the rank-1 count exactly - every rank-1 locus a zero-edit call), median 60.00, p10 14.42, p90 60.00, QV>=40 78.18% (was 77.41%), QV>=30 78.53%, QV>=20 79.42%; the non-rank-1 252 at median QV 14.67
- **Gate 4, the rival-dominant residue: 71 of 252** (interior-substitution 60 / twin-indistinguishable 7 / substitution-better-match 4 / zygosity-dosage 0 - the hypothesized dosage mechanism ABSENT; winner homozygous 0/71; 50 carry the pre-normalization signature); by census: in-axis 30+3 / neighbor 25+3 / foreign-repeat 3+1+1 of the interior-substitution class
- **The residue decomposition: 1,132 = 880 rank-1 + 71 rival-dominant + 181 seam/repeat-interior** (the 181 NOT addressable by geometry - the locality lever measured to convert only 9 of them; the 71 the irreducible copy-structure residue)
- **The sweep: 264,718,566 checks / 8 failure lines** (every failure the zero-regression guard's own naming of the 5 committed regression loci; four components closed gateregressed; the checker untouched, the goalposts unmoved)
- **Walls (arithmetic shown): the locality chain serial 3,086s + 4-wide 1,836s = 4,922s** (17/17, the .wall markers; the territory fleet's own chain: census 19,581s + anchor 7,268s + serial 2,504s + 4-wide 1,469s = 30,822s)

## The honest verdict for the whole arc
The measured chain: the windowed frame's broken domains (805/1,307 excluded, one molecule called, 4/502 recovered) were repaired by the realignment instrument (the truth pair in the universe at 1,133 expressible loci), the extent normalization (+327, the geometry that WAS real), and the alignment-induced locality domain (+4 net: 9 conversions against 5 named regressions - the strongest geometry candidate, built on real alignment, honestly measured small and two-sided). **The geometry-lever program is measured to exhaustion**: what remains (252 loci) is not addressable by geometry - 71 rival-dominant by the receipts' own both-placed evidence and 181 seam/repeat-interior where the winners place genuinely-better-matched repeat-family reads on real ortholog material. The next lever, if any, lives in the evidence model - a decision for the owner, with the honest scoreboard now standing at 880/1,132 = 77.7%, perfect calls 880, and every number above carried by committed receipts, identity gates, and a 264,718,566-check sweep whose only failures are the design's own loudly-named costs. Standing rules held to the last commit: truth assessment-side only; no thresholds; the scoreboard machinery unmodified; no product selection swap; no 502; stdbuf; the 64GiB guard clean everywhere; never from the repo root; never the pilot receipt commits. Final commit: 8e22841. Tree clean of tracked changes. THE ARC IS CLOSED.
